```sh
for r in jolly-roger missiv push-notification-server; do git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/$r fetch --all --prune; done
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/jolly-roger rev-parse --short origin/with/push-notifications
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/missiv ls-tree -r --name-only origin/main packages/client/src/lib | head -30
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/stratagems show alpha1-final:web/src/lib/stratagems/ui/missiv/missiv.ts | head -60
```

**Do** (preview branch `tf-F3/with/messaging`): 1. the dependency and the client constructed in the composition (one file and one spread, R1); 2. F3-2's stores wired, the panel logic-less; 3. F3-3; 4. F3-4; 5. F3-5 and the full e2e; 6. the node in `fanout.config.json`, `README.messaging.md`.

**Done when**: the node's verify passes; its full e2e is `with/push-notifications`' plus F3-5's cases; with missiv's env empty the app runs and says so; the diff against its stem is `hosted-account`-shaped (the spec's acceptance criterion: files added, one composition edit, a pane, env, e2e); every push approved.

**As built (2026-11-19)**, what a later step relies on (jolly-roger `tf-F3/with/messaging`, from `with/push-notifications` 4ff489e5; the config on `tf-F3/offshoot`):

- **`$lib/messaging/messaging.ts`**, `createMessaging({config, account, signOwnership, fetch?, createWebSocket?})`: one store of `{status: 'not-configured' | 'loading' | 'no-account' | 'unregistered' | 'registered', ...}` (from missiv's registration), `register()` (on first use; never rejects, a wallet's refusal is the state's `error`), `services` (missiv's `room` of `@<the app's host>`, `conversations`, `conversation(address, {read})` cached per address and mode, `accept`, `reject`) and `panel`. `@missiv/client`, `@missiv/common` (for `getConversationID`) and `missiv` ^0.4.0, devDependencies as every web dependency here. The wallet signature is F2's `publicationSigner` (connect 0.18's), so the wallet is asked once for notifications and messaging together. `$lib/messaging/sources.ts`: `messagingConfig(env, pageOrigin, namespace)` (`PUBLIC_MISSIV` an origin, missiv's domain the page's HOST, the app origin the page's origin; dynamic env, as F2's) and `messagingAccount(connection)` (missiv's account in lower case hex: its schemas refuse a checksummed address). The namespace is the app half's `'greetings'` (`app.ts`).
- **`$lib/messaging/panel.ts`**: the panel's state (which view, the inbox of the other members with the unread and request counts, the conversation shown and its reading mode, accept and reject with their failures) and `createComposer`. A request is shown with `read: false` (looking does not accept it; missiv's `markAsAcceptedAndRead` would), accepted by `accept`, then read as shown. The UI, `$lib/ui/messaging/` (six logic-less components), is ONE mount in `$lib/context/AcrossPages.svelte`: a fixed launcher and a vaul drawer in the drawer layer, rather than a navbar edit; the demo's rows get a "message this account" button.
- **F3-3 as built**: no trigger and no separate subscription: missiv pushes a DM to every device the recipient registered with "notify me", so "on by default once push is on" is a line in the toggle ("Direct messages to you are notified too", once the account is registered with missiv), not a second switch; opting out of DM pushes alone would need a missiv-side preference, which missiv does not have. The push server's `PUSH_TOKENS` lists missiv's token hash beside the trigger service's for the same app origin (`serve.ts`; push-notification-server accepts several entries per origin), and missiv's `PUSH_TOKENS` names the app's HOST (`localhost:5173=<token>`), which the push server normalises to the same origin.
- **The services**: `web/notifier/serve.ts` gains `missiv` (missiv's `createNodeServer`, rooms injected on the same port, 34007 by default, `NOTIFIER_MISSIV_PORT`; a libSQL file in the data directory), started by `all`; `keys.mjs` writes `NOTIFIER_MISSIV_PUSH_TOKEN`; a fourth zellij pane; `PUBLIC_MISSIV` empty in `web/.env`, `//:34007` in `.env.localhost`. The e2e script starts it on `E2E_MISSIV_PORT` (34107) with `E2E_MISSIV_PUSH_TOKEN`, and builds the app against it.
- **Tests**: server +3 files and +24 cases (122 / 1405 to 125 / 1429): the store against missiv's Node.js server in process (no port) with push-notification-server in process behind it, so a DM's push is checked from missiv's call to the recipient's device (with both tokens configured); the panel; the sources. Client +1 (10 / 58 to 11 / 59): with `PUBLIC_MISSIV` unset the panel says messaging is not configured. e2e `messaging.e2e.ts` on account 7 in TWO browser contexts (the burner generates its accounts per context, so one index gives two accounts and neither sends a transaction; one address added to `impersonate-addresses.json`, not the two "Since F2" foresaw), the conversation opened through `messaging.panel.openConversation` (what the demo's button calls; the recipient has no greeting to click). The room's socket is exercised only there (missiv's own store, tested in missiv).
- **The diff** (14 shared files, each in `[with/messaging]`): the composition (`app.ts`), the dependencies, the lockfile and `pnpmDepsHash`, the mount (`AcrossPages.svelte`), the demo page and `NotifyMe.svelte`, the env (`web/.env`, `web/.env.localhost`), the dev stack (`dev/zellij.kdl`, `web/notifier/serve.ts`, `web/notifier/keys.mjs`), the e2e (`scripts/run-e2e-tests.sh`, `impersonate-addresses.json`). `hosted-account`-shaped but for the panel's mount and the demo's opener, which a panel in the shell and "a player's address opening a DM" (F3-2) need.

### Step F4a (postponed): a table's end and its scores, in the base

Repo: template-commit-reveal `main`, cascaded to its six nodes (the games take it in F4c). Needs I1 and V1; independent of F1 to F3.

**What decides the shape** (measured 2026-11-16): the three games' table ends (above, and the analysis' "duplicated across the tree"); stratagems' client half (`score.ts`' `rankScores` and `settleBatches`, `table-end.ts`' live then recorded ranking, anyone-may-settle in batches, withdraw once settled, offline auto-settle) is about 150 to 190 generic lines; infinite-gambit's `_tally(budget)` is the resumable bounded count a large table needs. Tables are EIP-1167 clones made by `contracts/src/commit-reveal/CommitRevealGameFactory.sol` (`createGame`, a `GameCreated` event, a list `getGames`); etherfold follows a fixed list of contracts, so an indexer cannot follow every clone, but it can follow the factory. The commit-reveal rules apply (reveal order independence, a stake at risk, no persisted key or field renamed), and the gas budget is declared per deployment (ADR-0003).

**Decisions** (decided by the user on 2026-11-16: as proposed):

- **F4-1, what a high score is**: the score each game already computes at a table's end, one number per seat, recorded once when the table is settled; a game's high-score list is the best scores across all its tables (the top N, and each player's best). Rejected: the persistent world's scores (it never ends; stratagems' world leaderboard is its token economy); a score computed by a client (unverifiable).
- **F4-2, recorded on the factory**: a table's settlement calls `recordScores(seats, players, scores)` on the factory that made it, which accepts only its own clones and emits `ScoreRecorded(game, player, score, cycle)`; one contract for an indexer to follow, whatever the number of tables. Rejected: the table emitting it (one source per clone); a separate HighScores contract (one more address every deployment configures, when the factory already knows its clones). Item 1 measures the clone check's cost (a mapping written at `createGame`) against the commit budget.
- **F4-3, the settlement lifecycle in the base**: a table base with `_scoreOf(seat)` the game's hook, a resumable `_settle(budget)` (infinite-gambit's tally shape, anyone may call it after the end), the result recorded once, and `withdraw` reading the game's payout rule; the client half (`rankScores`, `settleBatches`, a table-end store) in `$lib/game/lobby`. The reference game is its first user (its score: its stake share at the end, measured in item 1). Rejected: only the event (each game keeps its settlement, and the record would be a fourth implementation of the same lifecycle).
- **F4-4, additive for the games**: the games adopt in F4c; until then their tables compile unchanged against the base (the hook is opt-in). Rejected: changing the three games here (three new deployments in a framework step).

**Start here.**

```sh
for r in template-commit-reveal stratagems reveal-or-die infinite-gambit; do git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/$r fetch --all --prune; done
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/template-commit-reveal show origin/main:contracts/src/commit-reveal/CommitRevealGameFactory.sol | sed -n 1,90p
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/template-commit-reveal show origin/main:contracts/src/commit-reveal/UsingCommitReveal.sol | sed -n 215,235p
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/stratagems show origin/main:contracts/src/game/table/StratagemsTable.sol | sed -n 1,80p
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/infinite-gambit show origin/main:contracts/src/game/internal/UsingGameTable.sol | sed -n 150,320p
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/reveal-or-die show origin/main:contracts/src/game/table/UsingGameTable.sol | sed -n 325,470p
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/stratagems show origin/main:web/src/lib/stratagems/score.ts | sed -n 1,60p
```

**Do** (preview branches `tf-F4a/<branch>` at template-commit-reveal; one commit per item): 1. measure the three settlements side by side, the clone check's gas, the reference game's score; 2. the contract base and the factory's record, test-first (a two-seat table, a tie, a resumed settlement, a refused foreign caller), the gas budget re-measured; 3. the client half, test-first, moved from stratagems' generic functions (the source SHA recorded); 4. the reference game on it, its table-end UI, one e2e case; 5. the cascade to the six nodes, verified, the e2e of `main`, `with/nft-identity` and `with/fuzd-pixi-indexer`.

**Done when**: a reference table settles in resumable rounds and its factory emits one `ScoreRecorded` per seat, once; a foreign caller is refused; the gas budget holds; the client half has the tests stratagems' had for those functions; the six nodes verify and the three e2e suites pass with the new case; the games are untouched and still verify against the new base; every push approved.

### Step F4b (postponed): high scores, indexed

Repos: template-commit-reveal `with/indexer` (and by cascade `with/fuzd-pixi-indexer`), `main` for the fallback. Needs F4a and F1b.

**What decides the shape**: etherfold has no aggregation primitive: a score is a field a handler writes, and a top-N is a query (`orderBy` and `first`), cheap on SQLite, a full scan in IndexedDB until two of etherfold's blocked backlog tasks land (`an-indexeddb-index-serves-the-accessor`, `the-browser-index-orders-a-u256-numerically`). A game's processor is the game's; the framework can give it a fragment to fold (entities and a handler for the factory's `ScoreRecorded`) the way `cycle-stamp.ts` relies on the game folding `CycleAdvanced`. `etherfold run` serves the same processor's `/graphql` from SQLite, and `etherfold build --publish` makes a snapshot a tab starts from.

**Decisions** (decided by the user on 2026-11-16: as proposed):

- **F4-5, a framework fragment**: `$lib/indexer/scores` exports the entities (`Score` per table and seat, `Best` per player) and the `onScoreRecorded` handler a game's processor spreads in, with the factory added to its source, and a `highScores` store reading the top N and the player's best. Rejected: a second processor for scores (two streams and two workers per game).
- **F4-6, read from the tab by default, from a hosted indexer when configured**: `PUBLIC_INDEXER_URL` set, the store reads that server's `/graphql` instead (the external tracker: an `etherfold run` of the same bundle, serving the global list from SQLite); R2 and R3: unset, the tab indexes as today. Rejected: server only (breaks the decentralised default); tab only (the top-N scan grows with every table; item 1 measures it at 1,000 tables).
- **F4-7, nodes without the indexer read the factory's logs** (`with/all`, so reveal-or-die and bomber-world): `getLogs` of `ScoreRecorded` from the factory's deploy block in bounded ranges, folded in memory. Rejected: requiring the indexer for a high-score list (a fast game would install etherfold for one page).
- **F4-8, achievements from high scores, and a beaten record notifying, are NOT in this plan**: once S2 exists (generic, with its reward action, the user's refinement of 2026-11-16) and F4b indexes `ScoreRecorded`, an achievement is a global rule trigger over that event (for example "a top-10 score") calling a reward target, and a beaten record a push; a step of its own, specified when F4c lands, with the reward service it needs. Rejected: adding it here (it would make F4 depend on F2 and S2, and no reward service exists).

**Start here.**

```sh
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/template-commit-reveal fetch --all --prune
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/template-commit-reveal show origin/with/indexer:web/src/lib/indexer/README.md | head -60
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/etherfold show origin/main:packages/accessor/src/types.ts | sed -n 55,75p
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/etherfold ls-tree -r --name-only origin/main work/tasks/backlog | head
```

**Do** (preview branches `tf-F4b/<branch>`): 1. measure the top-N in IndexedDB at 1,000 tables (a generated stream, the indexer test harness); 2. F4-5, test-first through `indexInProcess`; 3. the reference game's processor spreading it, a high-score page (logic-less); 4. F4-6 with a test against a GraphQL executor; 5. F4-7 at `main` (its own store, the same view); 6. the cascade, verified; the e2e of `with/indexer` and `with/fuzd-pixi-indexer` with one case (a settled table's scores on the page).

**Done when**: the page lists a settled reference table's scores on `with/indexer` (indexed) and on `main` (from logs); `PUBLIC_INDEXER_URL` switches the source, tested; item 1's figure is recorded; the nodes verify and their e2e passes with the new case; every push approved.

### Step F4c (postponed): the games record their scores

Repos: stratagems, infinite-gambit, reveal-or-die (bomber-world by cascade). Needs F4b.

**What decides the shape**: each game's score is already computed and settled its own way (above). Adopting F4-3 changes each game's table contract, so each is a NEW deployment: stratagems' D-live has not happened (free); reveal-or-die's last deployment is `deployment/rise-testnet` (2026-01-14, old); infinite-gambit has a Sepolia configuration (`config/sepolia`, in main).

**Decisions** (decided by the user on 2026-11-16: as proposed):

- **F4-9, each game's score**: stratagems, L4's net gems (`getScores`, already the table's argmax); infinite-gambit, a seat's material at the cap (`_materialOf`); reveal-or-die, what a survivor withdraws (its carried stake plus its leftover share), a dead seat's 0; bomber-world inherits reveal-or-die's. Rejected: one formula imposed by the base (a game's score is its rules).
- **F4-10, each game's settlement rebuilt on the base's lifecycle where it fits** (stratagems' and infinite-gambit's do; reveal-or-die's leftover pot is its payout rule behind `withdraw`), the game's own copy deleted; its divergence list and its omissions updated. Rejected: calling `recordScores` from each game's own settlement and keeping three lifecycles.
- **F4-11, one game per session**, stratagems first (it has the leaderboard the generic half came from), then infinite-gambit, then reveal-or-die with bomber-world. Rejected: all three in one session.

**Start here.**

```sh
for r in template-commit-reveal stratagems infinite-gambit reveal-or-die bomber-world; do git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/$r fetch --all --prune; done
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/stratagems show origin/main:web/src/lib/stratagems/leaderboard.ts | sed -n 1,40p
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/infinite-gambit show origin/main:docs/adr/0012*.md 2>/dev/null | head -30; git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/infinite-gambit ls-tree --name-only origin/main docs/adr/ | grep 0012
```

**Do** (per game, preview branch `tf-F4c/main`): 1. the stem merged; 2. the contract on the base, its tests unchanged as the oracle plus the record's, its gas pins re-measured; 3. its client on the generic half, its own copies deleted; 4. its high-score page (indexed for stratagems and infinite-gambit, from logs for reveal-or-die); 5. verify, contracts, the LAN build (`nix build git+file://$PWD#lan`, a deploy script changed), the full e2e.

**Done when**, per game: the contract tests pass unchanged plus the record's; the gas budget holds; the game's copies of the generic lifecycle are gone and its divergence list says what remains; its high-score page shows a settled table's scores in the e2e; verify, contracts, the LAN build and the e2e pass; every push approved.

### Step P1: the contracts page made copyable, from the rocketh playground

Repo: jolly-roger `main`, cascaded to its nodes. Needs nothing but the features' pushes (done). Added by the user on 2026-11-19 ("Yes move the improvement up"), from what the rocketh playground (wighawag/rocketh-playground, pull request 6) met when it copied jolly-roger's contracts page at 8a73e680 to read and call the contracts a reader deployed in the tab.

**What decides the shape** (measured 2026-11-19 at jolly-roger `origin/main` 8a73e680, the commit the playground copied; re-derive it):

- **Duplicate ids.** `web/src/routes/contracts/components/FunctionInputs.svelte:49,58,64,74` gives each input `id="input-<index>"`, and the page renders one `FunctionInputs` per function card, so two functions with arguments both have an `input-0`: a label then focuses the other card's input, and `getByLabel` is ambiguous. The playground added an optional `idPrefix` (default `''`, the ids unchanged) and passes the contract address and function name.
- **Logic in a `.svelte` file.** The same file's `$effect` (`:26-41`) is the validation loop, which AGENTS.md puts in `.ts`. The playground moved it to `web/src/routes/contracts/lib/input-errors.ts`, `syncInputErrors(inputs, values, errors)`, the component only calling it. jolly-roger skips `address` there because `AddressInput.svelte` validates itself; the playground renders a plain input and validates addresses with the other types, so the skip has to become a parameter for both to share one file.
- **An unused import.** `web/src/routes/contracts/components/ContractFunction.svelte:18` imports `Address.svelte` and never renders it.
- **The read is not separable from the wallet.** `web/src/routes/contracts/lib/contractCall.ts` holds `readContractValue` (wallet-free: a `PublicClient`, the ABI item, the address, the raw inputs) beside `executeContractWrite`, and the module imports `@etherplay/connect`, `$lib/core/transaction` and `$lib/core/connection`, so the read cannot be copied without the wallet layer. The playground re-wrote it.
- **The ethereum UI reaches the app layer.** `web/src/lib/core/utils/ethereum/blockExplorer.ts:1-2` imports `$env/static/public` (`PUBLIC_USE_INTERNAL_EXPLORER`) and `$lib/deployments-store` at module level; `Address.svelte` and `TransactionHash.svelte` (and `AddressInput.svelte` through `Address`) call it, so `core/ui/ethereum/` cannot be used outside jolly-roger's composition, and the playground did not copy those three components. ENS is already a capability (`core/capabilities/ens.ts`, optional, inert without a provider), so ENS is not the blocker; the explorer is. Its other users: `web/src/routes/explorer/lib/utils.ts`.
- **Format helpers with no web3 in them.** `web/src/lib/core/utils/format/hex.ts`, `json.ts` and `error.ts` import nothing; `value.ts` imports viem, `balance.ts` nothing but is a token amount. jolly-roger tests all five (`web/test/lib/core/utils/format/`).

**Decisions:**

- **P1-1, the three small fixes as the playground built them** (decided by the user on 2026-11-19): `idPrefix` on `FunctionInputs` (default `''`), the contracts page passing `<address>-<function name>-`; the validation loop in `web/src/routes/contracts/lib/input-errors.ts` as `syncInputErrors(inputs, values, errors, options?)` with `options.selfValidated?: (input) => boolean` (jolly-roger's `FunctionInputs` passes `(input) => input.type === 'address'`, the playground passes nothing), so the playground's copy of that file becomes byte-identical; `ContractFunction`'s unused import removed. Rejected: leaving the skip hard-coded (the playground's copy then differs forever in one line).
- **P1-2, the read in a module of its own** (decided by the user on 2026-11-19): `readContractValue` moves to `web/src/routes/contracts/lib/contractRead.ts`, importing only viem and `./utils`; `contractCall.ts` re-exports it, so no caller changes. Rejected: a `PublicClient` built inside the read (the caller owns the transport; the playground's is the node in the tab).
- **P1-3, DECIDED 2026-11-20 (as refined by "The P1 session's recommendations" below): the block explorer as a capability.** `core/capabilities/block-explorer.ts`, optional like ENS: `provideBlockExplorer({txUrl(hash), addressUrl(address), internal: boolean})`, `useBlockExplorer()`; `blockExplorer.ts` becomes pure functions of a chain info and the flag (no `$env`, no store); jolly-roger's root provides it from `$lib/deployments-store` and `PUBLIC_USE_INTERNAL_EXPLORER`; `Address`, `TransactionHash` and the explorer's `utils.ts` read the capability, and with none provided `linkTo="auto"` resolves to no link. Effect: `core/ui/ethereum/` copies as it is into an app with no explorer and no ENS (the playground, which would then show addresses and hashes with jolly-roger's own components). Rejected: a `chain` prop on every `Address` and `TransactionHash` (every call site on `main`, in the connection flow, the navbar, the modals, the explorer and the demo, threading the same value); leaving it module-level (the ethereum UI never leaves jolly-roger's composition).
- **P1-4, DECIDED 2026-11-20 (as refined by "The P1 session's recommendations" below): the format helpers' home is NOT this step.** `hex.ts`, `json.ts` and `error.ts` (with their tests) belong at the root, template-svelte, and `balance.ts` and `value.ts` stay at jolly-roger; that is a svelte-line seam and a root cascade, so it is recorded in K3's list rather than done here, where it would make a jolly-roger step cascade from the root. Rejected: moving them in P1 (a cascade through every node of the svelte line for three files).
- **P1-5, DECIDED 2026-11-20 (as refined by "The P1 session's recommendations" below): the cascade.** jolly-roger's own nodes (every branch of `origin/offshoot:fanout.config.json`), each verified, the e2e of `main` and `with/embedded-chain` (the contracts page's cases); below jolly-roger the change rides the next cascade that crosses (K1's), named in its entry. Rejected: a cascade of its own through template-commit-reveal and the games for these files (P-2: each cascade carries one thing, and this one is small enough to ride).
- **P1-6, the playground follows** (decided by the user on 2026-11-19): once pushed, a pull request on rocketh-playground (asked for, as every push) re-copies the changed files from the new jolly-roger commit, naming it in each header: `FunctionInputs.svelte`, `ContractFunction.svelte` (its read half), `input-errors.ts` (then identical), and, with P1-3, `Address.svelte`, `TransactionHash.svelte` and what they import, in place of the playground's own address and hash display. Out of the tree, so its verify is the playground's `pnpm verify`. **Extended (2026-11-19, with P1-7)**: once P1-7 is pushed, the playground's four explorer copies (`blockScanner.ts`, `transactionDecoder.ts`, `transactionItem.ts`, `lib/utils.ts`) are re-copied from the new commit too, naming it in each header.
- **P1-7, DECIDED 2026-11-20 (as refined by "The P1 session's recommendations" below): the explorer's decoding made copyable** (added 2026-11-19 by the agent that built rocketh-playground pull request 7, https://github.com/wighawag/rocketh-playground/pull/7, branch `feat/transactions-view`, 1c03ecb, which copies four explorer files from jolly-roger 8a73e680 at their paths, each taking the chain or the contracts as a parameter; each copy's header lists its changes; NOT yet re-measured by the plan: P1's session re-derives every line number and caller below against `origin/main`). What the store is used for, as reported at 8a73e680: `web/src/routes/explorer/lib/services/blockScanner.ts` only `deployments.get().chain.id` in `getChainId()` (`:39-41`), the localStorage keys of the block index, reached only when `useIndex` is true; `services/transactionDecoder.ts` only `deployments.get().contracts` in the private `findContractByAddress` (`:60-72`), called once from `decodeTransaction` (`:378`); `lib/utils.ts` the same, in the exported `findContractByAddress` (`:32-45`), used by `decodeLogs`, and it re-exports four helpers of `blockExplorer.ts` (`:298-303`). The change, as the playground built it, so its copies become identical to jolly-roger's but for their headers: `blockScanner.ts` drops the store import and `getChainId()`, its six index helpers and `scanBlocksWithIndex` take `chainId` first, `scanLatestTransactions` takes a trailing `chainId?: number` (required when `useIndex` is true, throwing without it), and `scanBlocksInBatches` is exported (the playground pages through older blocks with it); `transactionDecoder.ts` drops the store import, exports `type DecodingContracts = Record<string, {address: string; abi: unknown}>` (the shape of `deployments.get().contracts`), and takes it in `findContractByAddress(address, contracts)` and `decodeTransaction(tx, receipt, publicClient, contracts)`; `services/transactionItem.ts`' `loadTransactionItemData(tx, publicClient, contracts)` passes them on; `lib/utils.ts` drops the store import, takes the same type in `findContractByAddress(address, contracts)` and `decodeLogs(logs, contracts)`, and loses the `blockExplorer` re-export (P1-3's refinement (c), so this part lands with P1-3 or after it). jolly-roger's callers pass `deployments.get().contracts` read at call time (P1-3 (b)); at 8a73e680 they include `stores/transactionDetails.ts`, `stores/addressData.ts`, `stores/transactionList.ts` (which also passes a `chainId` from its options when the block index is on), `TransactionItem.svelte` and `TransactionView.svelte` (`findContractByAddress` at `:456`), the session finding every one. Test-first: `decodeTransaction` and `decodeLogs` against a contracts map with no store mocked, and `scanLatestTransactions` with `useIndex` and no `chainId` throwing; the existing tests in `web/test/routes/explorer/` unchanged, as the oracle (the playground carries them byte for byte). Rejected: the store read through a default parameter (the import stays, so the module still does not copy); a module-level setter (hidden global state, and two chains on one page could not coexist).
- **P1-8, DECIDED 2026-11-20 (as refined by "The P1 session's recommendations" below): a refused replay explains itself.** `decodeTransactionError` returns viem's `shortMessage`, which hides the node's explanation: a replay the node refuses (webevm's -32000 "historical state not available", a block below its `stateHistory` window) reaches the UI as "Missing or invalid parameters. Double check you have provided the correct parameters." It prefers the deepest cause's `details`. Rejected: keeping `shortMessage` (the user cannot tell a refused replay from a bad call).
- **P1-9, DECIDED 2026-11-20 (as refined by "The P1 session's recommendations" below): an unknown call is not a transfer.** `formatDecodedTransaction` labels any undecoded transaction without a function name "Transfer", a call with data to a contract no deployment knows included; it shows such a call as "Call to an unknown contract" with its 4-byte selector, as the playground does, and "Transfer" only for a transaction with no data. Rejected: keeping the label (it names a call a value transfer).
- **P1-10, DECIDED 2026-11-20 (as refined by "The P1 session's recommendations" below): arguments named and typed, numbers unquoted.** `formatDecodedArgs` prints numbers in quotes (`incBy("5")`) because `toPlainJson` turns a bigint into a string before formatting; it names and types each argument from the ABI item (`incBy(by: 5)`), as the playground's `namedArgs` and `formatArg` do (`web/src/lib/playground/transactions/decode.ts` in pull request 7). Rejected: only unquoting the numbers (the names are in the ABI item, and a positional list of several arguments is unreadable). Each of P1-8 to P1-10 is decided on its own; only the accepted ones are built, each test-first.

**Checked by the K1 session (2026-11-19)**, at the user's request, against `origin` (jolly-roger `main` still 8a73e680; rocketh-playground pull request 6 merged, `main` bb944a5):

- Every measurement above holds (the four `input-<index>` ids, the `$effect` at `:26-41`, the unused import at `:18`, `contractCall.ts`' wallet imports, `blockExplorer.ts`' two module-level imports).
- **Nothing in the tree depends on the ids**: no e2e or unit test of any node selects `input-<n>` or uses `getByLabel` on the contracts page (the only `getByLabel` is the navbar's "Open menu"), so `idPrefix` breaks no suite. No node below jolly-roger modifies `FunctionInputs.svelte`, `core/ui/ethereum/`, `core/utils/ethereum/` or the explorer's files; only bleeps modifies the contracts page (`+page.svelte`, and `ContractFunction.svelte`, which keeps the unused `Address` import).
- **P1-1's shared function keeps jolly-roger's behaviour only if a self-validated input's error is DELETED, then skipped** (jolly-roger's loop does `delete errors[key]; continue`); the playground's copy has no such branch, so it stays byte-identical only once it takes the new file whole.
- **For P1-3, recommended refinements** (the decision itself still the user's): (a) defined with `defineCapability` like ENS (`useENS`) and provided in `routes/+layout.svelte` beside `provideRoute` and `provideENS`, which already reach `Address.svelte` (it calls `useRoute()` and `useENS()` today); (b) the provided functions read `deployments.get()` at call time rather than a snapshot, so HMR and a changed store behave as now (an embedded world still has no explorer, as now); (c) `routes/explorer/lib/utils.ts` only RE-EXPORTS four helpers; the readers are `AddressView.svelte` and `TransactionView.svelte`, which take the capability and the re-export goes; (d) `Address.svelte.test.ts` and `TransactionHash.svelte.test.ts` stop mocking `$env/static/public` and provide the capability, and `TESTING.md`'s example changes with them; (e) `AddressInput.svelte` becomes copyable with them (it renders `Address`). Cost below jolly-roger: `+layout.svelte` is modified by template-commit-reveal (one hunk beside `provideRoute`), reveal-or-die, bleeps and mandalas, so the provider line conflicts there in K1b, a one-line resolution each.
- **P1-5 against K1, now specified and decided**: P1 is built and pushed first, cascaded to jolly-roger's nine nodes; K1a then starts from them, and K1b's cascade carries P1 below jolly-roger (K1-8 and K1-12 name its files and `contracts.e2e.ts`), so no cascade of P1's own crosses template-commit-reveal.

**Re-measured by the P1 session (2026-11-20)**, against `origin` (jolly-roger `main` still 8a73e680, so P1-1's and P1-2's measurements hold as the K1 session found them; rocketh-playground `main` d2b2158, pull request 7 NOT merged, `feat/transactions-view` 1c03ecb):

- **The cascade inside jolly-roger is conflict-free**: none of its eight other nodes is behind `main`, and none modifies the contracts page, the explorer, `core/ui/ethereum/`, `core/utils/ethereum/`, `routes/+layout.svelte` or their tests (`git diff --stat origin/main origin/<branch>` on those paths is empty for all eight).
- **P1-7's report holds line for line at 8a73e680** (`getChainId()` at `:39-41` and its six callers; the private `findContractByAddress` at `:60-72` and its one call at `:378`; the exported one in `lib/utils.ts` at `:32-45`, used by `decodeLogs` at `:81`; the re-export at `:298-303`), and pull request 7's four copies differ from jolly-roger's in exactly the changes their headers list. The callers that change: `stores/transactionDetails.ts:62,64`, `stores/addressData.ts:60`, `services/transactionItem.ts:28` (through `TransactionItem.svelte:44`), `TransactionView.svelte:456`, `stores/transactionList.ts:63`. **One correction**: `transactionList.ts` passes NO chain id (its options are `publicClient` and `useBlockIndex`), and the client it gets is built with the chain (`core/connection/remote.ts:275`, `createPublicClient({chain: chainInfo, ...})`). The playground calls `scanBlocksInBatches` only, never the index.
- **The copies declare `DecodingContracts` twice** (`transactionDecoder.ts` and `lib/utils.ts`), beside the two `ContractInfo` interfaces and two `findContractByAddress` that jolly-roger already duplicates.

- **P1-8**: viem 2.55's `BaseError` copies a cause's `details` up the chain, so the top error's `details` is the node's own message ("historical state not available") while its `shortMessage` is `InvalidInputRpcError`'s generic text; `decodeTransactionError` returns `e.shortMessage` at its last `BaseError` branch, after the raw-data walk.
- **P1-9**: `decodeTransaction` returns `{isDecoded: false}` with no function name in THREE cases (no data; data to an address no deployment knows; data a known contract's ABI does not decode), and `formatDecodedTransaction` names all three "Transfer". The existing test ("labels a plain transfer when there is no function name") carries no data hint, so it stays green while "Transfer" stays the label of a transaction with no selector.
- **P1-10**: viem's `decodeFunctionData` gives positional args, `toPlainJson` makes each bigint a decimal string, and `formatArgValue` quotes a string: `incBy("5")`. Event args are shown as JSON (`TransactionView.svelte:501`), already named. `formatDecodedArgs`' four tests pass bigints, not strings, so a second optional parameter leaves them green.
- **P1-3**: `blockExplorer.ts` is read by `Address.svelte` and `TransactionHash.svelte`, and through `lib/utils.ts`' re-export by `AddressView.svelte` (`:28-29`, `:137-138`) and `TransactionView.svelte` (`:31-33`, `:166-167`). No component of jolly-roger is mounted imperatively (no `mount(` under `web/src`), so a context capability reaches every `Address`. Out of P1's scope but the same shape: `core/ui/faucet/index.ts` imports `$env/static/public` and `$lib/deployments-store` at module level too.

**As built (2026-11-20)**, on preview branches `tf-P1/<branch>` of jolly-roger and `tf-P1/copies-from-jolly-roger` of rocketh-playground; where it differs from the decisions above, this is what was built:

### Steps K1a to K1c: jolly-roger's core goes home, the main-page seam, the indexed state

Repos: jolly-roger (K1a); template-commit-reveal, reveal-or-die, bomber-world, stratagems, infinite-gambit, template-zk-game, duel-in-the-dark, bleeps, mandalas (K1b); template-commit-reveal's `with/indexer` line, stratagems, infinite-gambit (K1c). voxlings read only. Specified 2026-11-19 by the session that met K1 unspecified, and decided by the user the same day ("regarding your plan I agree"). K1a starts after P1 is pushed (the user added P1 before K1 that day), so K1b's cascade carries P1's files below jolly-roger too (P1-5).

**What decides the shape** (measured 2026-11-19; every node of the tree level with its stem but voxlings, 67 behind `with/nft-identity-fuzd`):

- **The world connection's fix is jolly-roger's, carried in template-commit-reveal since 2026-09-22.** template-commit-reveal `main` a85c37b6 modifies exactly two `lib/core` files of jolly-roger `integration` f81a5759: `core/connection/types.ts` (+23: `EstablishedConnection.nodeURL`, the url the factory actually used) and `core/connection/remote.ts` (+15 -3: `establishConnectionOn` reports `nodeURL`; `establishRemoteConnection` takes an optional `deployments`, the same chain with the game at another address, which a table needs, 88dee31b). Its consumer is in `context/core.ts` (1126f860, 37 lines there: `connectionNodeURL` feeds the local signer's transport, the payment rail and the ledger's node, in place of `PUBLIC_NODE_URL`), mixed in that file with template-commit-reveal's own table, revealer and delegation-pair changes. Its finding (`work/notes/findings/a-worlds-signer-broadcast-to-the-apps-node.md`, "the fix belongs upstream in jolly-roger") says why nothing saw it: it needs a configured `PUBLIC_NODE_URL`, a local signer and a world that is not the app's, and no test pins it (template-commit-reveal has no test naming the transport). In jolly-roger, `remote.ts` and `types.ts` at `integration` are `with/embedded-chain`'s byte for byte (both already in `[with/embedded-chain]`'s list: `establishConnectionOn` and the worlds are that branch's), and `context/core.ts` is already in `[integration]`'s list (where the local signer meets the world). So the bug is live at `integration` (an embedded world, a local signer, and the e2e script's `PUBLIC_NODE_URL`), in template-zk-game (which carries `integration`'s files unchanged; its offline route is omitted, so latent there) and in duel-in-the-dark below it. `integration` has no offline e2e suite.
- **The `GAME` seam exists already, in template-zk-game** (6d2b1a19, 2026-10-30): its `$app-specific` module (`web/src/lib/game/app.ts`) exports `GAME = {name, route}`, which its navbar and home page import; `web/e2e/routes.ts` exports `GAME = {route, heading, homeLink, contract}`, which `home`, `hydration` and `overlays` read; `web/test/lib/game-world.ts` exports `GAME_SCRIPTS` and `GAME_CONTRACT`, which the embedded world's two tests read. jolly-roger names its demo literally in each (`/demo/`, "Check The Demo", `GreetingsRegistry`, `deployGreetingsRegistry`), so template-zk-game modifies five shared files for constants only: `web/test/lib/embedded/deployments.test.ts`, `world.test.ts` (also its contracts package's name in the rocketh import), `web/e2e/tests/hydration.e2e.ts`, `overlays.e2e.ts` and `home.e2e.ts`; and `routes.ts`, `navbar.svelte` and `+page.svelte` partly. template-commit-reveal does the same for `deployments.test.ts` (`Game`), `hydration.e2e.ts`, `navbar.svelte` (`Play`, `/play/`) and `routes.ts`; duel-in-the-dark for `routes.ts` and one line of `world.test.ts`; bleeps for `hydration` and `overlays` (small); voxlings for six. The games below template-commit-reveal modify none of them (they inherit `/play/`). An export added to the `$app-specific` contract must exist in EVERY such module, or `svelte-check` fails where the inherited navbar imports it: template-commit-reveal `placement/game.ts`, reveal-or-die and bomber-world `world/game.ts`, stratagems `stratagems/game.ts`, infinite-gambit `chess/game.ts`, voxlings `voxlings/game.ts` (none exports `GAME`), template-zk-game and duel-in-the-dark (both do), bleeps and mandalas (their own modified `context/app.ts`).
- **The contracts page's write is a second, larger seam, solved twice differently**: template-commit-reveal's `web/e2e/fixtures/contracts-page.ts` (`WRITE_CONTRACT`, `WRITE_FUNCTION`, `selectContract`, 2026-09-11) and template-zk-game's (`HARMLESS_CONTRACT`, `HARMLESS_WRITE`, `sendHarmlessWrite`, 2026-10-30), each modifying `contracts.e2e.ts`, `stalling-wallet.ts`, `escape-hatch.e2e.ts` and `pending-operation.e2e.ts` (template-commit-reveal: 424 lines added, 214 removed).
- **`indexed-state.ts`** (template-commit-reveal `with/indexer`, 289 lines; inherited unchanged by `with/fuzd-pixi-indexer`, stratagems and infinite-gambit): F1-11 measured about 120 lines as `createIndexed`'s plumbing. It has NO test of its own; it is exercised through its two callers, the reference game's `placement/indexer/board.ts` (`test/lib/placement/indexer.test.ts`) and stratagems' `board/indexed-board.ts` (six of its tests); infinite-gambit calls it nowhere.

**Decisions** (proposed 2026-11-19 and DECIDED by the user the same day: "I agree"):

- **K1-1, three steps, as C1a and C1b were**: K1a at jolly-roger (the homes and their cascade to its nine nodes), K1b below it (one cascade carrying both of K1a's changes), K1c the indexed state at template-commit-reveal `with/indexer` once K1b has landed. Rejected: one step (about forty merges in one session, and a red below jolly-roger would hold jolly-roger's pushes); the `nodeURL` fix and the `GAME` seam in two cascades (two passes over every node for two changes that touch disjoint files and are of one kind: a divergence going home).
- **K1-2, the fix's home: `remote.ts` and `types.ts` at `with/embedded-chain`, taken from template-commit-reveal byte for byte, and the consumer at `integration`** (`context/core.ts`' `connectionNodeURL` hunks, the lines of 1126f860). Both files are `with/embedded-chain`'s divergences already and `core.ts` is `integration`'s, so no list grows; template-commit-reveal's merge then takes the stem's two files and drops their entries. Rejected: `main` (`with/embedded-chain` rewrote `establishRemoteConnection` around `establishConnectionOn`, so every hunk would conflict there, and no `main` node has a second world); all three at `integration` (two more entries in its list for files that are `with/embedded-chain`'s); the `deployments` option left below (it is generic, a connection describing records that are not the build's, and it is in the same hunk).

Rejected: moving the fix untested (template-commit-reveal's has no test, and its finding says the development configuration takes the right branch for the wrong reason).
- **K1-4, the hosted case stays named, not solved**: under `walletOnly: false` a missing url is fatal while a world has none; no node combines `with/hosted-account` with an embedded chain, so the comment moves with the fix. Rejected: deciding it blind.
- **K1-5, the `GAME` seam at jolly-roger `main`, in template-zk-game's names and shape** (its NAMES superseded on 2026-11-20 by K1-13: `MAIN_PAGE`; the shape stands): `context/app.ts` (`$app-specific`) exports `GAME = {name: 'Demo', route: '/demo/', ...}` with the home page's button label as a field, read by `navbar.svelte` and `routes/+page.svelte`; `web/e2e/routes.ts` exports `GAME = {route, heading, homeLink, contract}`, read by `SMOKE_ROUTES`, `home`, `hydration` and `overlays`. Its doc comment says it names the app's main page (a demo here, a game below). Rejected: a non-game name (`MAIN_PAGE`: template-zk-game and duel-in-the-dark would rename theirs, conflicting on every importer, for a word); a shared constants file outside `$app-specific` (renaming a page would still modify a shared file, the divergence the seam removes).

Rejected: at `main` (the tests are `with/embedded-chain`'s); in `web/e2e/routes.ts` (Node unit tests importing an e2e module).

P1 rides this cascade below jolly-roger (P1-5): its files are jolly-roger `main`'s contracts page, `core/ui/ethereum/`, `core/utils/ethereum/blockExplorer.ts`, `core/capabilities/`, `core/ens/` (the types split out), the explorer's views, stores and services, `$lib/index.ts` (the explorer's composition) and `routes/+layout.svelte` (P1's "As built"; measured 2026-11-20: template-commit-reveal, reveal-or-die, bomber-world, stratagems, infinite-gambit and bleeps modify `$lib/index.ts` and `+layout.svelte`, mandalas `+layout.svelte`, and template-commit-reveal `main` merges `tf-P1/integration` with no conflict), which template-commit-reveal (5 hunks, one beside `provideRoute`), reveal-or-die, bleeps and mandalas modify, and bleeps also modifies the contracts page (`+page.svelte`, `ContractFunction.svelte`, which keeps the unused `Address` import P1-1 removes): expect those conflicts in K1b.

Test-first: a test file of its own (none exists), with the three behaviours the rebuild gains (progress closed on stop, no update after stop, the gate holding the worker) red before. Rejected: the callers moved onto `createIndexed` directly (every game's board edited); leaving it (a second copy of the plumbing, drifting on every cascade of `indexed.ts`).
- **K1-12, the measure**: every node's verify against its baseline (with `OFFSHOOT_STEM_REFS` as the prompt says); the full e2e of the nodes whose behaviour changes: jolly-roger `main`, `with/embedded-chain`, `integration`, template-commit-reveal `main` and template-zk-game (K1a, K1b), template-commit-reveal `with/indexer` and stratagems (K1c); on every other node only the suites K1 edits (`home`, `hydration`, `overlays`, the smoke routes) and, in K1b, `contracts.e2e.ts` for P1's page, three at a time. Rejected: a full e2e of all 27 nodes (about four hours, for constants and a fix whose files are already the games' byte for byte).

- **K1-2**: `remote.ts` and `types.ts` at `with/embedded-chain` are template-commit-reveal `main`'s byte for byte, pinned by `test/lib/core/connection/world-node-url.test.ts` (5 cases, 3 red before). The consumer at `integration` is template-commit-reveal's `core.ts` hunks (the `connectionNodeURL` reported by the factory, to the payment rail and to `buildChainConfig`), with two of its comments rephrased to drop a spaced hyphen and the hosted case's parenthesis made true of jolly-roger ("this branch has no wallet host, `with/hosted-account` has no embedded chain"): K1b resolves template-commit-reveal's `core.ts` to these lines.
- **K1-3**: the unit case is `test/lib/context/world-node-url.test.ts` at `integration`: with `PUBLIC_NODE_URL` mocked, a factory that reports no url hands none to `resolveSignerRpc` and to `createPaymentRail` (spied through `vi.doMock`), and the remote world hands both the app's url; red on `origin/integration` 9286e08a (both got the app's url), green after. **No e2e case**: a throwaway `/offline-demo/` case showed the greeting never reaches the local signer in a browser, before or after the fix. The world's connection stops at `WalletConnected`, because at `integration` it must sign in and the route mounts no connection flow to answer that, so the send spins forever (finding `the-offline-demo-cannot-sign-in-at-integration` on jolly-roger `work`). And a visible consequence of the fix: under a configured `PUBLIC_NODE_URL` the offline demo no longer believes it has an app RPC, so its messages wait for the wallet ("Connect to load messages"), as a dev run without the url already showed.
- **K1-6**: `web/test/lib/game-world.ts` re-exports `config` and `extensions` from `jolly-roger-contracts/rocketh/config.js` and exports `GAME_SCRIPTS` and `GAME_CONTRACT`; `deployments.test.ts` came out byte for byte template-zk-game's, and its `world.test.ts` differs only in its rocketh import, which its `game-world.ts` takes over in K1b.

- **The test** (`web/test/lib/indexer/indexed-state.test.ts`, 10 cases, server project): the indexer's port and progress readable are FAKED (`vi.mock` of `@etherfold/browser` and `@etherfold/graphql/worker`), the follower is the real one over a spec whose reads the test answers one at a time, so a read or a stamp can land after the store stopped. Six cases pin what the store did before (the stamp under Manual and Timed, the tip's block on a quiet chain, a refusal, a stop, a failed read keeping the last state, a re-read for the player, no worker outside a browser); four were red on `origin/with/indexer` (K1-11's three gains, the in-flight one split in two: a read, and a stamp waiting for its block's timestamp). The real fold stays `test/lib/placement/indexer.test.ts`'s, which passes unchanged. Rejected: an in-process chain for this file (the timing a stop races against cannot be held there).
- **The rebuild**: `createIndexedState` keeps its options and store and takes ONE new optional option, `gate` (the app's `chainFetchGate`), passed to `createIndexed` with the rest (`player` as its `account`); the store subscribes `createIndexed`'s single value and maps it onto the seam: progress to `progress`, a read or a progress move to a stamped `publish`, a `query` error onto `status.error` (cleared by the next publish, as before), a refusal or a stop onto `{loading: false, error}`, after which nothing is published (before, a later progress tick could have). A `run` counter drops a stamp still waiting for its timestamp when the last subscriber leaves. 289 lines to 252, most of what remains its doc comment and its options' types. Neither board passes `gate` yet (the skeleton's poll does, `fetchGate: core.chainFetchGate`): a K3 item, since it changes when a board starts.
- **What did not move**: jolly-roger's `indexed.ts` still says template-commit-reveal's store "may later be rebuilt on this one", now done (a K3 item at its home; editing it here would be a divergence).

```sh
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/template-zk-game show origin/main:web/test/lib/game-world.ts; git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/template-zk-game show origin/main:web/e2e/routes.ts | sed -n 20,45p
```
