**Done when** (K1a): every jolly-roger node has its stem's preview head as an ancestor and verifies against its baseline plus K1-3's and K1-6's cases; K1-3's case was red on `origin/integration`; `remote.ts` and `types.ts` at `with/embedded-chain` are template-commit-reveal `main`'s byte for byte; no divergence list grows; the checks clean; the e2e of K1-12 matches its baseline; every push approved. (K1b): template-commit-reveal `main` lists no `lib/core` file; template-zk-game's five constant-only files equal the stem's; every node below jolly-roger in K1-8's scope verifies against its baseline, its checks clean, its e2e of K1-12 matching its baseline; the finding closed; every push approved. (K1c): `indexed-state.ts` is built on `createIndexed` and its new test was red on the three gains before; the two callers unedited and green; the cascade's nodes verify and their e2e of K1-12 matches its baseline; every push approved. All three: no lockfile change is expected (none adds a dependency), so no `pnpmDepsHash` moves, and if one does it is recomputed as the prompt says; slot 0's ports free before and after.

### Steps K2a to K2c: the offline world's shared boot

Repos: jolly-roger (K2a); template-commit-reveal (K2b); reveal-or-die, bomber-world, stratagems, infinite-gambit (K2c). voxlings read only. Specified 2026-11-21 by the session that met K2 unspecified, and decided by the user the same day ("I approve").

**What decides the shape** (measured 2026-11-21 on the `origin` heads: jolly-roger `with/embedded-chain` e9c3e018, `integration` 80762ffd; template-commit-reveal `main` 8bb83556; reveal-or-die bf2c6655, bomber-world 2a140e20, stratagems ec7d6548, infinite-gambit 46aa4815, voxlings 68e985cd):

- **Seven copies of one world, at three levels.** `web/src/lib/offline.ts` is 332 lines at jolly-roger (`with/embedded-chain`, and `integration` byte for byte, which lists no divergence for it), 1307 at template-commit-reveal `main`, 1169 at reveal-or-die, 1220 at bomber-world, 1097 at stratagems, 1696 at infinite-gambit (beside its own `offline-boot.ts` 308, `offline-presets.ts` 525, `offline-preset-setup.ts` 194) and 762 at voxlings. Every game lists it as a modified shared file ("K2: the shared boot" in template-commit-reveal's and reveal-or-die's reasons). Within template-commit-reveal it differs per node too: `with/nft-identity`, `with/all` and `with/nft-identity-fuzd` +141 -97 (an avatar per player: the deploy script, the stake and `offlineIdentityOf`), `with/indexer` and `with/fuzd-pixi-indexer` +25 (`forgetPreviousWorldIndex`, which stratagems carries again).
- **What is the same everywhere** (split by top-level declaration and compared per game): `openWorld` (about 90 lines: the remembered chain id, the coherence probe in the worker, a new id on an incoherent restore, `createEmbeddedWorld` over the worker node) is one function in all seven copies, jolly-roger's included, but for four parameters: the deployment (scripts, `data`, accounts, balances, environment, `expectedContracts`), the chain's properties, the provision hook, and voxlings' data override. The boot's memo and status (`status`, `offlineWorld`, `pending`, `boot`), `worldChain`, `connectOfflinePlayer` and `stopAnnouncingOfflineWallet` are byte-identical in at least three of the four games. `$lib/game/lobby/offline-table.ts` (310 lines, `seatTheOfflineTable`) is ALREADY shared, byte for byte in every game: the precedent this step follows.
- **What each game varies, in `buildOfflineWorld`** (312 lines at the template, every copy different): the identity a seat plays as (an address; an avatar; reveal-or-die's per table mode; bomber-world's per SEAT, its character, the hook the analysis named); what a seat is staked with, in the world and at a table (a walk of the table every copy repeats, around one call); what the game reads off its opened table (reveal-or-die's mode, stratagems' board bounds); the played seats' loop and strategy (`createOfflinePlayers`, `playedSeatDecider`); and steps of its own around the template's: funding the browser's key (reveal-or-die and stratagems both, the template not), forgetting the previous world's index (`with/indexer`, stratagems), settling a table once ended (stratagems, at start), deploying every army before the board shows and the demo presets (infinite-gambit, a different chain-id source and opener for a preset). The games also LAG the template: reveal-or-die and bomber-world have no hotseat and no `playedSeatDecider` (their own levels), since their copies predate them; the shared lobby already detects what a world offers by its exports (`LOCAL_HUMANS_AT_MOST`, `AI_LEVELS`, `startOfflineTable`), so a shared boot changes nothing a game does not export.
- **What is not a copy**: `offline-players.ts` (522 to 726 lines per game, `createOfflinePlayers` 455 lines at the template) is each game's commit and reveal loop; its machinery is shared already (`$lib/game/core/played`, "WHAT IS NO LONGER HERE IS THE MACHINERY").
- **The finding at jolly-roger `integration`** (`the-offline-demo-cannot-sign-in-at-integration`, on `work`): the demo's world takes the app's `TARGET_STEP` (`SignedIn` at `integration`) and nothing answers the sign-in, nor registers the derived key. template-commit-reveal's world does both itself: `connectOfflinePlayer` (connect, then `requestSignature()` when the target is `SignedIn`: generic) and `authoriseTheBrowsersKey` (`$lib/game/acquire/authorise.ts`, 180 lines, template-commit-reveal's own file, no test of its own; it names no game and needs only `connection`, `delegation`, `accountExecutor`, `publicClient` and `credits`, all of which jolly-roger `integration`'s context has, from `with/local-signer`'s `ui/delegation`, `ui/credits` and `onchain/delegation`). At `with/embedded-chain` the demo connects at its first send (`routes/demo/lib/setGreeting.ts`, `ensureConnected()`), which works there because the target is `WalletConnected`. Neither jolly-roger node has an `/offline-demo/` e2e case.
- **voxlings is 118 behind `with/nft-identity-fuzd`** (67 at K1): left to its own next cascade, as V1 and K1 left it.

**Decisions** (proposed 2026-11-21 and DECIDED by the user the same day: "I approve"):

- **K2-1, three steps, as K1 was**: K2a at jolly-roger (the world's generic opener at `with/embedded-chain`, the sign-in and key authorisation at `integration`, the finding closed), K2b at template-commit-reveal (the commit-reveal boot's steps in `$lib/game/offline/`, its `offline.ts` reduced to its composition, cascaded to its nodes), K2c the games (each `offline.ts` reduced to its composition in its merge of K2b). Rejected: one step (four design merges below a refactor at two levels, and a red game would hold jolly-roger's pushes); the template level alone (the opener is jolly-roger's in all seven copies, and the finding's fix is jolly-roger's).
- **K2-2, the opener at jolly-roger `with/embedded-chain`, in `web/src/lib/offline-world.ts`** (new, beside `offline-node.ts`): `openOfflineWorld({chainIdKey, deployment, chain, provision, onStep, storage?})` (the remembered id, the probe, the new id on an incoherent restore, the world over `offlineWorldNode`), `createOfflineBoot(build)` (the status readable, the memo, a failure as `Failed` with a retry), and `connectTheWorld(connection)` (template-commit-reveal's `connectOfflinePlayer`); jolly-roger's own `offline.ts` rebuilt on them, its exports unchanged. Every storage key and database name stays the caller's or the same string (they are WIRES: a persisted world must restore across the change, bomber-world's `:v2` included). Rejected: inside `$lib/embedded` (the mechanism does not know the app's worker or the `offline-world-*` names, so every caller would pass the same worker node); left per game (the one function all seven copies share).
- **K2-3, the demo's world connects at boot on every jolly-roger node** (`connectTheWorld` in the opener's caller, as template-commit-reveal's does), so `with/embedded-chain` shows its account at once rather than at the first send. Rejected: connecting at boot at `integration` only (a larger divergence at `integration` for the same behaviour).
- **K2-4, the finding's fix: the world signs in and authorises its key itself at `integration`** (option one of the finding): `authoriseTheBrowsersKey` moved up to `integration` as `web/src/lib/ui/delegation/authorise-here.ts`, from template-commit-reveal's `authorise.ts` with its game words made the app's, test-first (already registered, registered by one transaction, refused without a signer: fakes of the five members it takes); `integration`'s `offline.ts` calls it after `connectTheWorld`, one divergence entry with `core.ts`' reason (where the local signer meets the world). Pinned by an `/offline-demo/` e2e case at `with/embedded-chain` (a greeting sent offline) inherited by `integration`, where it runs under the e2e script's `PUBLIC_NODE_URL` and was red on `origin/integration` (it hangs at `WalletConnected`): K1-3's end-to-end case of the node url fix. The finding closed. Rejected: the demo's world targeting `WalletConnected` at `integration` (smaller, but the offline demo would then never exercise the local signer, the thing K1a's fix is about); `with/local-signer` as the module's home (it has no world, so nothing there calls it).
- **K2-5, the commit-reveal half as STEPS, not a spec with hooks**: `$lib/game/offline/` (template-commit-reveal's own, new) exports deep steps a game's `buildOfflineWorld` composes: the table opened and composed on its records (around `seatTheOfflineTable`, returning the opened table for the game to read its mode or board off), the humans seated (connect, authorise, the hotseat sign-in loop, the local revealer, the handover), the table walk that stakes every seat (taking a per-seat stake and passing the SEAT INDEX, which is bomber-world's hook), the played seats resolved (taking `identityOf({player, seat, game})`), and the ready status whose `start` runs the context, the played seats, the revealer and the handover, with a game's own extra starts. `offline.ts` keeps the game's deployment, stake, identity, strategy and a short `buildOfflineWorld` (an estimated 150 to 300 lines per game instead of 1100 to 1700, measured by K2b). Rejected: one `createOfflineGame(spec)` with hooks (the measure above already needs eight: identity, two stakes, the opened table, the loop, a pre-boot step, a post-authorise step, extra starts, and infinite-gambit's presets replace the chain-id source and the opener; each new variation would widen it, where a step-composing game just calls one more function).
- **K2-6, behaviour unchanged, the existing tests the oracle**: `world.test.ts`, `offline-players.test.ts`, `offline-lobby.test.ts`, `offline-table.test.ts`, `offline-tables-boundary.test.ts` and every offline e2e suite pass unedited at every node (only their imports may move); the new steps get their own node tests (the table walk with a fake env, the opener's restore and refusal), test-first where they gain behaviour. A game keeps what it offers: no game gains hotseat or AI levels by the extraction. Rejected: levelling the games' features in the same step (reveal-or-die's hotseat is a design question of its own).
- **K2-7, `offline-players.ts` stays per game**: not in K2. Rejected: extracting the loop (it is each game's commit and reveal; the machinery is shared already).
- **K2-8, infinite-gambit's chess offline-table suite stays its backlog task** (`chess-e2e-on-the-lean-rules-board`): K2c keeps its node tests of the offline table as the oracle. Rejected: writing it in K2c (a chess e2e is a feature's work, not a refactor's).
- **K2-9, `forgetPreviousWorldIndex` goes to template-commit-reveal `with/indexer`** as a step there (it is byte-alike in stratagems), so stratagems drops its copy in K2c. Rejected: to jolly-roger `with/indexer` (it has no offline world).
- **K2-10, the scope and the measure**: K2a jolly-roger's nine nodes, K2b template-commit-reveal's nine, K2c reveal-or-die, bomber-world, stratagems, infinite-gambit; not voxlings (118 behind and the user's live work; it gets K2 with its next cascade, whose `offline.ts` then meets K2b's steps). Every node's verify against its baseline (with `OFFSHOOT_STEM_REFS`); full e2e of the nodes whose offline world is rebuilt: jolly-roger `with/embedded-chain` and `integration`, template-commit-reveal `main`, `with/nft-identity` and `with/indexer`, and the four games; on every other node the offline suites and `layout-shell`, three at a time. Rejected: offline suites only on the games (their `offline.ts` is rewritten whole, and only the full suite crosses a table, the hotseat and a restore together).

**Start here** (K2a; K2b and K2c the same, with the step's own repos).

```sh
for r in jolly-roger template-commit-reveal reveal-or-die bomber-world stratagems infinite-gambit voxlings; do git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/$r fetch --all --prune; done
for rb in "jolly-roger with/embedded-chain" "jolly-roger integration" "template-commit-reveal main" "reveal-or-die main" "bomber-world main" "stratagems main" "infinite-gambit main" "voxlings main"; do set -- $rb; echo "$1 $2 $(git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/$1 rev-parse --short origin/$2) $(git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/$1 show origin/$2:web/src/lib/offline.ts | wc -l)"; done   # 332 332 1307 1169 1220 1097 1696 762 on 2026-11-21
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/jolly-roger diff --quiet origin/with/embedded-chain origin/integration -- web/src/lib/offline.ts && echo "integration has with/embedded-chain's offline.ts"
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/jolly-roger grep -n 'ensureConnected' origin/with/embedded-chain -- web/src/routes/demo/lib/setGreeting.ts
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/template-commit-reveal show origin/main:web/src/lib/game/acquire/authorise.ts | grep -n '^import'
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/jolly-roger show origin/work:work/notes/findings/the-offline-demo-cannot-sign-in-at-integration.md | head -8
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/voxlings rev-list --count origin/main..stem/with/nft-identity-fuzd    # 118 on 2026-11-21
ss -ltn | head -40
```

**Do, K2a** (preview branches `tf-K2a/<branch>` from each jolly-roger node's `origin/<branch>`, the plan on `tf-K2a/work`; one commit per item, a merge counting as its own): 1. re-derive; every node's verify as the baseline. 2. At `with/embedded-chain`: the opener's tests (red), `offline-world.ts` (green), `offline.ts` rebuilt on it with `connectTheWorld` at boot (K2-3), and the `/offline-demo/` e2e case. 3. The cascade to `integration`; there `authorise-here.ts` test-first, `offline.ts`' call and its divergence entry, the e2e case red on `origin/integration` and green. 4. The cascade to `with/push-notifications` and `with/messaging` (and nothing to the nodes without a world); every verify, `check-divergences`, `check-omissions`, `check-stem-drift`; the e2e of K2-10. 5. The finding closed on `tf-K2a/work`.

**Do, K2b** (preview branches `tf-K2b/<branch>`; the stem's names are K2a's "As built": `openOfflineWorld`, `createOfflineBoot`, `connectTheWorld` in `$lib/offline-world`, `authoriseTheBrowsersKey` in `$lib/ui/delegation/authorise-here`): 1. re-derive (`git merge-tree` of `main` against `integration`'s pushed head names `offline.ts`, the new modules, their tests, the offline e2e case, the two READMEs and the lists, else stop); 2. the merge, template-commit-reveal's `connectOfflinePlayer` and `authorise.ts` taken from the stem (`game/acquire/index.ts` re-exporting the stem's); 3. `$lib/game/offline/`'s steps, test-first where they gain a test; 4. `offline.ts` rebuilt on them, the oracle unedited; 5. the cascade to its eight other nodes (`with/nft-identity` keeping only its identity, stake and deploy script; `with/indexer` given K2-9's step); every verify and check, the e2e of K2-10.

**Do, K2c** (the steps' names are K2b's "As built"; each game's `test/lib/app-world.ts` gains its `config`, `extensions` and `APP_SCRIPTS` for the stem's `offline-world.test.ts`, and its divergence list's reason for `offline.ts` is rewritten): one merge per game of its stem's K2b head, its `offline.ts` rebuilt in the merge on K2b's steps with its own variations (reveal-or-die's table mode and funded key, bomber-world's per-seat identity through the walk's seat index and its `:v2` key kept, stratagems' board bounds, funded key and settling with K2-9's step for its index, infinite-gambit's presets and opening deployments), each game's oracle unedited; every verify and check, the full e2e of each.

**Done when** (K2a): `openWorld` exists once at jolly-roger (`offline-world.ts`), `offline.ts` at `with/embedded-chain` built on it; the `/offline-demo/` case passes on `with/embedded-chain` and `integration` and was red on `origin/integration`; `authorise-here.ts`'s cases were red before; no divergence list grows but `integration`'s by `offline.ts`; the finding closed; the checks clean; every push approved. (K2b): template-commit-reveal's `offline.ts` has no `openWorld`, `connectOfflinePlayer` or `authoriseTheBrowsersKey` of its own and is built on `$lib/game/offline/`; its oracle unedited and green on all nine nodes; `with/nft-identity`'s diff of `offline.ts` against `main` is its identity, stake and deploy script only; every push approved. (K2c): no game's `offline.ts` defines `openWorld`, the boot's memo, the humans' seating or the table walk; every game's oracle unedited and green, its full e2e matching its baseline; every push approved. All three: no lockfile change expected, so no `pnpmDepsHash` moves (if one does, it is recomputed as the prompt says); slot 0's ports free before and after.

**As built (K2a, 2026-11-21)**, on preview branches `tf-K2a/with/embedded-chain` and `tf-K2a/integration` of jolly-roger; where it differs from the decisions above, this is what was built (K2b builds on these names):

- **K2-2, the opener's interface**: `openOfflineWorld({chainIdKey, deployment, chainProperties?, provision?, onStep?, storage?})` in `web/src/lib/offline-world.ts`. `deployment` is `OfflineWorldDeployment`, the `EmbeddedWorldSpec` fields a copy varies (`rocketh`, `scripts`, `accounts`, `environment`, `data`, `initialBalances`, `expectedContracts`). The parameter K2-2 called `chain` is `chainProperties` (a game passes `config.chains[31337].properties`): all seven copies name the chain `Offline` in Ether and override `averageBlockTimeMs: 1000` and `finality: 0` the same way, so the opener fixes those four (`OFFLINE_CHAIN_TIMING`, with the finality comment) and spreads the caller's properties under the two. `provision` is the mechanism's hook told `chainId` too (`ProvisionParams & {chainId}`), which every copy closed over. The id is remembered with `rememberChainId` under `chainIdKey` and re-minted there on an incoherent restore, so a game's own re-mint (template-commit-reveal's refused table) keeps writing the same key before it builds again. The database names stay `offline-world-deployments:<id>` and `offline-world-chain:<id>`, the copies' own.
- **K2-2, the boot**: `createOfflineBoot(build)` where `build(booting, ...args)` reports through `booting(what)` and returns what `Ready` carries; it returns `{status, start(...args), current()}`, `status` an `OfflineBootStatus<Ready>` readable, `start` joining a boot in flight whatever it is given and never rejecting (a failure is `Failed` and drops the memo), `current()` the boot for `letGoOfWorld`. The status is set to `Ready` by the boot once `build` returns, not by `build`. jolly-roger's `OfflineWorldStatus` is `OfflineBootStatus<{world, context}>`, structurally the type it was.
- **K2-3**: `connectTheWorld(connection)` takes `Pick<Context['connection'], 'ensureConnected' | 'requestSignature' | 'targetStep'>` and throws what the connection throws; `offline.ts` catches it (not fatal, as template-commit-reveal's). At `with/embedded-chain` the change is not visible on the page (the demo read its messages before, since a world's provider is readable), so the e2e case pins it through the console handle (`globalThis.offlineWorld.context.connection` at its `targetStep` before any click), red on `origin/with/embedded-chain`'s `offline.ts`.
- **K2-4**: `authorise-here.ts` exports `authoriseTheBrowsersKey`, `AuthorisationOutcome` and `AuthorisationDeps` under template-commit-reveal's names, so its `game/acquire/` can re-export the stem's in K2b; it imports `topUpCeiling` from `ui/credits/top-up-flow` (the `ui/credits` barrel does not export it). `integration`'s `offline.ts` calls it once after `connectTheWorld` (no retry loop: template-commit-reveal's `authoriseWhenReady` is its hotseat's, for an account switch), not fatal, its refusal logged. The e2e case was red twice at `integration`: on `origin/integration` (stuck before `SignedIn`) and on the merge of the boot connect alone (signed in, the greeting never sent). Its 5 cases (not K2-4's three: also the account that cannot send, and a reverted registration).
- **K2-10's "cascade to `with/push-notifications` and `with/messaging`"**: none. Their stems are `with/indexer` and `with/local-signer`, which K2a does not touch, and neither has a world; their heads, and those of `main`, `with/local-signer`, `with/hosted-account`, `with/indexer` and `website`, are unchanged, so their verify was not re-run (it would run the same commit).

**As built (K2b, 2026-11-21)**, on preview branches `tf-K2b/<branch>` of template-commit-reveal's eight changed nodes and of jolly-roger `with/embedded-chain` and `integration`; where it differs from the decisions above, this is what was built (K2c builds on these names):

- **A fix at its home first**: jolly-roger's `offline-world.test.ts` (K2a) opened its world as the deployer alone, so at template-commit-reveal, whose game is owned by the `admin` named account, its first deploy failed (`admin` unresolved). It now deploys as `embedded/world.test.ts` does, the deployer and the admin, both funded (`with/embedded-chain` 6fb0ef84, merged into `integration` 08a02496; both verifies their K2a counts). template-commit-reveal `main` merges `integration` twice (K2a's a4da0953, then 08a02496).
- **The stem's tests read `test/lib/app-world.ts`**, which at template-commit-reveal exported only `APP_CONTRACT`; it now also exports `config`, `extensions` (its contracts package) and `APP_SCRIPTS` (its four deploy scripts, at their default data: every contract the app's records name, or the world's own check refuses the deploy), and at `with/nft-identity` the avatars' script before the game (one entry in its divergence section). **K2c meets the same**: each game's `app-world.ts` needs its own `config`, `extensions` and scripts, or the stem's `offline-world.test.ts` fails at its first deploy.
- **The merge**: `web/e2e/tests/offline-demo.e2e.ts` omitted (its route, `/offline-demo/`, is), with its reason; `$lib/game/acquire/authorise.ts` deleted, the rail's `index.ts` re-exporting `$lib/ui/delegation/authorise-here`'s three names; `connectOfflinePlayer` replaced by `connectTheWorld`.
- **K2-5's steps**, all in `web/src/lib/game/offline/` (barrel `index.ts`, a README): `createOfflineWallet()` (`announce({provider, chainId, table, gas})`, the wallet one account per person here and the gas of every seat and of the hotseat's revealer; `current()`; `stop()`), `giveGas`; `stakeEverySeat({table, you, humans?, stake})`, the walk, `stake` told `{player, seat, occupant}` (the seat index is bomber-world's hook); `resolvePlayedSeats({table, identityOf})`, `identityOf` told `{player, seat}`; `openTheWorldsTable({world, wallet, lobby, table, at, stake, chainIdKey, storageKey, onStep?, storage?})`, answering `{kind: 'table', table, records, establishConnection}` (the `table` is the `ResolvedTable` a game reads its mode or board off) or `incoherent` having itself let go of the world (wallet stopped, world disposed, a new id remembered, the table's record forgotten) so the game only builds again; `theWorldsGame(world)`, the same shape for a world that is not a table; `revealerForTheHumans` (before the context), `seatTheHumans({context, wallet, humans, identityOf, onStep?})` (connect, sign in, authorise, the hotseat's loop, answering who is seated), `handOverBetweenThem`; `readyToPlay({context, players, revealer?, hotseat?, decider?, also?})`, `also` being a game's own starts (K2-5's "extra starts"); `holdTheWorldOnTheConsole`; `worldChain`, `worldReader`. A game's step before the boot or after the authorisation is a call between these, as K2-5 meant. Their tests: `test/lib/game/offline/{seats,ready,humans}.test.ts`, 10 cases, red before the modules existed.
- **K2-9's step** is `web/src/lib/game/offline/world-index.ts` at `with/indexer` (`forgetPreviousWorldIndex({prefix, chainId, storage?, deleteIndex?})`, `INDEXED_CHAIN_ID_STORAGE_KEY`), imported by its path rather than through the barrel, so `with/indexer` modifies no file of `main`'s for it; its test 4 cases, red first. `with/indexer`'s divergence reason for `offline.ts` was stale ("the offline world's indexer worker") and now says what the file differs by.
- **`offline.ts`** at `main`: 1307 lines to 846, its exports and every wire unchanged (`OfflineWorldStatus` is `OfflineBootStatus<{world, context}>`, structurally the type it was). The doc comments of what stays the game's are kept, which is most of the 846 (K2-5's "150 to 300" was its code): so `with/nft-identity`'s merge conflicted in one hunk only (its stake at the table inside the walk), and its `offline.ts` differs from `main`'s by its identity, its stake and its deploy script, as before. `.offshoot-divergences`' reason for `offline.ts` at `main` ("K2: shared with the games") is left for K2c, whose merges change the games' lists anyway.
- **One behaviour difference, unobservable**: `stopAnnouncingOfflineWallet` now also forgets the wallet (the old one kept a stale reference, read by nothing before the next provisioning replaced it).

**As built (K2c, 2026-11-22)**, on preview branches `tf-K2c/main` of reveal-or-die, bomber-world, stratagems and infinite-gambit, with fixes at their homes on `tf-K2c/<branch>` of jolly-roger `with/embedded-chain` and `integration` and of template-commit-reveal's eight changed nodes; where it differs from the decisions above, this is what was built:

- **Three fixes at template-commit-reveal `main`, in `$lib/game/offline`** (K2b's steps did not offer what two games compose): `seatTheHumans` takes `afterAuthorising`, a game's own step for each person once their key is authorised (after the attempt when one person is here), not fatal: stratagems funds every person's browser key there, in hotseat as alone, and reveal-or-die its one (K2b's "a step after the authorisation is a call between these" holds for one person only, not inside the hotseat's loop); `revealerForTheHumans` and `handOverBetweenThem` take the game contract's deployment name (`contract`, `Game` when omitted): stratagems' is `Stratagems`, and a table's records name no `Game`; `createOfflineWallet({announce})` takes the game's own announcer, the mechanism's by default: infinite-gambit's demo preset announces with a fixed mnemonic (its own `embedded/wallet.ts` divergence). Test-first (`humans.test.ts` +4, `wallet.test.ts` new, 2; red on `origin/main`), every existing caller unchanged, cascaded to the eight other nodes by clean merges.
- **One fix at jolly-roger `with/embedded-chain`, in `offline-world.test.ts`** (K2a's, found when stratagems first ran it): a descendant whose deploy names other accounts exports `APP_ACCOUNTS` from `test/lib/app-world.ts` (optional; the deployer and the admin otherwise), each funded, since the world's accounts REPLACE the config's and stratagems' scripts name `tokensBeneficiary` and `timeOwner`; and the restore is checked by the hash of the block the first open ended on rather than by the height, since stratagems' generator `update` runs on every deploy by design and grows a restored chain by a block. Merged into `integration`, then into template-commit-reveal `main` and its nodes.
- **Each game's `app-world.ts`** names its own contracts package and deploy scripts (reveal-or-die's four, bomber-world's are reveal-or-die's byte for byte since it uses `reveal-or-die-contracts`, stratagems' six with its `APP_ACCOUNTS`, infinite-gambit's four), listed as a divergence where the game had none for it (reveal-or-die, infinite-gambit).
- **reveal-or-die** (1169 lines to 883): its deployment, `offlineIdentityOf(player, mode)`, the table's mode read off the opened table (`tableModeAt`), the per-seat stake in the world and at a table (avatar purchase, or `join` from the seat's own account) inside `stakeEverySeat`, its key funded in `afterAuthorising`, the table closed by the tab as `readyToPlay`'s `also`, the played seats through `resolvePlayedSeats` (which carries the level, so its `withLevels` went). `playedByTheWorld(table, mode)` stays, synchronous, because the oracle calls it so. No hotseat, as before: `humansAtThisDevice` is one person, so the steps build no revealer and no handover.
- **bomber-world** (1220 lines to 936): reveal-or-die's file taken whole in the merge, its one variation put back: `offlineIdentityOf(player, seat, mode)`, the seat index from the walk (`stake`'s `seat`) and from `resolvePlayedSeats`' `identityOf`, `seatIndexOf` for the labels and the person, and `CHAIN_ID_STORAGE_KEY` `:v2` kept. Its diff of `offline.ts` against reveal-or-die's is that and nothing else.
- **stratagems** (1097 lines to 771): its deployment and stake, the `Stratagems` contract named to the revealer and the handover, the table's board read off the opened table, every person's key funded in `afterAuthorising`, the table settled by the tab as `also`, the decisions on the console handle, and K2-9's `forgetPreviousWorldIndex` from template-commit-reveal `with/indexer` (its own copy and key gone, the key the same string). The table's stake keeps its one allowance for every unstaked seat: the walk collects them, the allowance and the stakes follow.
- **infinite-gambit** (1696 lines to 1313): its deployment, stake and opening deployments as before; the demo preset keeps its own opener (never persisted, so nothing to restore; its chain named as the opener names one, on `OFFLINE_CHAIN_TIMING`), gets its own wallet (`createOfflineWallet` with the fixed-mnemonic announcer) and a boot of its own per take (`createOfflineBoot`, a restart being a new take whose build first lets go of the old world), and the page's `offlineWorld` is the preset's status while a preset is chosen, the player's world's otherwise (the old file shared one status between the two). `playing` (the advance poked only while the board plays) is an `also` start, counted once.
- **What stays per game, as K2-5 meant**: each `offline.ts` defines no opener, no boot memo, no sign-in or key authorisation, no hotseat loop and no table walk; what remains is each game's deployment, stake, identity, contract name, strategy and its own steps between the template's. The remaining loops over seats are each game's synchronous `playedByTheWorld`, kept for its oracle (stratagems' and infinite-gambit's `OfflinePlayer` also carry the seat's address, which `resolvePlayedSeats` does not).
- **voxlings** meets all of this in its next cascade (K2-10): its `offline.ts` rebuilt on these steps, and its `app-world.ts` given its contracts package, its scripts and, if its deploy names accounts other than the deployer and the admin, `APP_ACCOUNTS`.

### Steps K3a to K3e: the seams and the lists, level by level

Repos: template-svelte, template-svelte-tailwind, template-svelte-shadcn (K3a); template-svelte-tailwind-blog, conquest-website-2, ronan-eth, wabisabi-website, arts, palestine, conquest-demo, template-svelte-docs, rocketh-website (K3b); jolly-roger (K3c); template-commit-reveal, reveal-or-die, bomber-world, stratagems, infinite-gambit (K3d); template-zk-game, duel-in-the-dark, bleeps, mandalas (K3e). voxlings read only. Specified 2026-11-22 by the session that met K3 unspecified, and decided by the user the same day ("I approve push").

**What decides the shape** (measured 2026-11-22 on the `origin` heads: template-svelte `main` cee769b, template-svelte-tailwind ad9437c, template-svelte-shadcn 197c124, template-svelte-tailwind-blog d585cbd, jolly-roger `main` d43a1b79, `with/indexer` 35d8375f, `with/push-notifications` ec2f51a9, template-commit-reveal `main` 6da1bdf4, `with/indexer` f47eeef5, infinite-gambit 27d2fc15, stratagems 32f46b18, template-zk-game 0c283ef8, voxlings 68e985cd; every main checkout clean but wabisabi-website's, dirty with exactly the `fallback-release.json` K3 names, and voxlings', two untracked idea notes):

- **The tree is 39 nodes; 17 have no list of their own.** Every node is level with its stems except template-zk-game, 14 behind `integration` (K2a's commits: K2-10 left it out) and so duel-in-the-dark with it, and voxlings, 156 behind `with/nft-identity-fuzd`. No list: the root (none needed), template-svelte-tailwind (7 modified shared files), template-svelte-shadcn 7, template-svelte-tailwind-blog 6, conquest-website-2 17, ronan-eth 21, wabisabi-website 10, arts 8, palestine 2, conquest-demo 12, template-svelte-docs 12, rocketh-website 11, voxlings 67; carrying their stem's list byte for byte (the check's "no list of its own", exit 2): template-zk-game (`integration`'s, 26 modified), duel-in-the-dark (template-zk-game's, 23), bleeps (jolly-roger `main`'s, 45), mandalas (42). Every jolly-roger and template-commit-reveal node and the four games keep their own. (Counts are files differing from every stem that has them, as `check-divergences.mjs` counts; most of a leaf's are its README, AGENTS.md, `package.json`, lockfile, `app.css`, `web-config.json` and icon.)
- **The notification panels are rewritten at two levels, not every level**: template-svelte-tailwind rewrites both `core/notifications/Notifications.svelte` (root 238 lines; +33 -164) and `core/service-worker/VersionAndInstallNotfications.svelte` (285; +33 -198) in Tailwind UI classes; template-svelte-shadcn rewrites both again (+29 -99, +41 -93) on its own components (`$ui/card`, `$ui/alert`, `core/ui/button`), and already split the paint out: `Notifications.svelte` builds each notification's actions and renders `NotificationCard.svelte` (new at shadcn, with `NotificationClasses` in `types.ts`, and `NotificationOverlay.svelte`). jolly-roger and every node below carry shadcn's unchanged. So a root change to either file conflicts twice, and shadcn paints with components, not classes: a class map cannot express it. The root has no browser test project (node tests only), so its paint is checked by `svelte-check` and the build, and seen in a browser first at jolly-roger's e2e.
- **The shadcn layer patches**: jolly-roger `main` lists six: shadcn's `dialog-`, `drawer-`, `popover-` and `select-content.svelte` portal into `$lib/core/ui/layers` (`layers.ts` 132 lines, `test/lib/core/ui/layers.test.ts` 160, which checks the list against `app.css`' `--z-layer-*` scale), `NotificationOverlay.svelte` (shadcn's own file) and the root's `components/NavigationProgress.svelte` drop their z-index. The seven layers are generic but one, `system`, whose reason cites jolly-roger's ADR-0004 (modals derived from domain state). The containers are rendered by jolly-roger's `+layout.svelte` from `LAYERS`. shadcn's descendants (arts, palestine, conquest-demo, template-svelte-docs, rocketh-website) would inherit them; template-svelte-docs modifies `+layout.svelte` and `app.css`.
- **The root's push items** (F2's): `core/service-worker/index.ts:520` adds `event.data` as the notification where the worker posts `{type, notification}` (one line, jolly-roger `with/push-notifications` fixes and lists it); `web/src/service-worker/index.ts` (408 lines) declares the Declarative Web Push payload inline and parses it in `handlePush`, untested; `with/push-notifications` replaces that by `$lib/push/payload.ts` (`notificationFromPush`, `notificationTarget`, tested), typed by `@push-notification/server`'s `DeclarativePushNotification`. `core/service-worker/push-notifications/` (index 315, utils 14, README 46) is wired to nothing at the root and omitted by `with/push-notifications`.
- **The format helpers**: jolly-roger `main`'s `core/utils/format/hex.ts` 23, `json.ts` 18, `error.ts` 61 import nothing (tests 29, 105, 75); `index.ts` re-exports them with `balance.ts` and `value.ts` (viem), read through the barrel by five files. The root has no `core/utils/format`. rocketh-playground carries the three copied.
- **The blog's posts source**: `lib/types.ts` 15, `routes/api/posts/+server.ts` 33, `routes/feed.xml/+server.ts` 194, all three modified by both sites for the same five things: the glob (`/src/posts/*.md` against conquest-website-2's `/src/posts/**/index.md`), the slug (the file name against the folder), the publish rule (`published` true at the blog's api, `!== false` at its feed and at ronan-eth, `!draft` at conquest-website-2: the analysis' inconsistency), the post's URL (`/blog/` against `/devlog/`) and the `Post` fields. template-svelte-docs' `core/docs/feed.ts` (62 lines, `escapeXML` and `renderFeed`) shares about 40 lines with the blog's feed, through no common ancestor but template-svelte-tailwind.
- **wabisabi-website's `build`** is `node scripts/fallback-release.ts && vite build`, which rewrites the tracked `src/lib/data/fallback-release.json` from GitHub (its main checkout is dirty with it right now); `pages.yml` documents the step; `site.ts` and `github-release.test.ts` import the committed file.
- **jolly-roger's items**: the contracts page's write suites (`contracts.e2e.ts`, `escape-hatch.e2e.ts`, `pending-operation.e2e.ts`, `fixtures/stalling-wallet.ts`, all from `main`) name `GreetingsRegistry` and `setMessage` literally; template-commit-reveal's `fixtures/contracts-page.ts` 148 lines (`WRITE_CONTRACT`, `WRITE_FUNCTION`, `selectContract`, `writeForm`, `executeButton`, carried by reveal-or-die, stratagems and infinite-gambit) and template-zk-game's 106 (`HARMLESS_CONTRACT = MAIN_PAGE.contract`, `HARMLESS_WRITE`, `openContract`, `writeCard`, `sendHarmlessWrite`), each modifying the four files (template-commit-reveal +1229 -368 across seven files with its game fixtures, template-zk-game +193 -221). `MAIN_PAGE.contract` of `web/e2e/routes.ts` has no reader at jolly-roger yet. `core/ui/faucet/index.ts` imports `$env/static/public` and `$lib/deployments-store` at module level (read by `navbar.svelte` and `InsufficientFundsModal.svelte`). The explorer's `TransactionItem.svelte:144` and `TransactionView.svelte:132` print "Contract Call" themselves. "the game" prose: `lib/embedded` 23 lines in 9 files (`with/embedded-chain`), `lib/indexer` 21 in 4 and its tests 35 in 2 (`with/indexer`, `worker-choice.test.ts`' `const GAME` among them), `scripts/run-e2e-tests.sh:295` (every node), `indexed.ts:29-30`' "may later be rebuilt", `web/e2e/routes.ts:4-6`' "Here it is the greetings demo".
- **template-commit-reveal's and the games' items**: `routes/+page.svelte:37-39` and `home.e2e.ts:69` name `/play/` and "Online" literally (reveal-or-die, bomber-world and stratagems carry them unchanged, infinite-gambit modifies both); `main`'s list reason for `offline.ts` still says "K2: shared with the games"; `placement/indexer/board.ts` and stratagems' `stratagems/board/indexed-board.ts`, the only two callers of `createIndexedState`, pass no `gate`; infinite-gambit's hover seam is +66 lines in three framework files (`game/core/commit-reveal-game.ts` an optional `hover` part fed only while ready, `game/render/events.ts` a `hovered` event, `game/render/input.ts` its tracking) and its two tests (`input.svelte.test.ts` +38, `canvas2d.svelte.test.ts` rewritten, 228 lines).
- **Not the tree's, or not yet**: the splash (reveal-or-die's `ui/loading/splash.ts` 153 and `SplashScreen.svelte` 146, bomber-world one colour) was left out of `with/pixi-js` on purpose: its `LoadingGate.svelte` says it gates the surface and not the app because a full-screen splash means editing `+layout.svelte`, and "brand stays with the game" (its Decision 2). The Router fix is `solidity-proxy@0.5.2`'s `Router10X60` (infinite-gambit's observation `router-loses-selectors-past-48`, vendored and pinned by `RouterSelectors.test.ts`): its home is that package, outside the tree. F1-12's middleware: still only stratagems publishes a processor bundle (`vite.processor-bundle.ts`), so it stays.

**Decisions** (proposed 2026-11-22 and DECIDED by the user the same day: "I approve push"; each the decision, then what it rejects):

- **K3-1, five steps, one per level of the cascade, as K1 and K2 were**: K3a the svelte line's top (template-svelte, tailwind, shadcn), K3b its nine leaves, K3c jolly-roger, K3d template-commit-reveal and the four games, K3e jolly-roger's other children. Each root and shadcn change crosses every node below, so they land together in K3a and travel in ONE merge per node (K3b, K3c, then K3d and K3e), each level adding its own items to the same merge's step. K3b and K3c both need only K3a pushed (the leaves and jolly-roger do not meet), K3d and K3e only K3c. Rejected: one step (39 nodes); a step per item (each root item a cascade through 38 nodes, against P-2).
- **K3-2, the notification paint split from its behaviour at the root, in shadcn's shape**: the root's `Notifications.svelte` and `VersionAndInstallNotfications.svelte` keep the behaviour (which notifications, which actions, the update and install states, their transitions and live region), the action lists computed in a `.ts` beside them (`core/notifications/view.ts`, tested in node: AGENTS.md's logic-less rule), and both render ONE paint file, `core/notifications/NotificationCard.svelte` (`{notification: {title, body?, icon?}, actions: {label, onClick, primary?}[], class?, classes?}`, shadcn's props), the root's styled with its own `<style>`. tailwind and shadcn each modify only `NotificationCard.svelte` (one entry each: "the card painted in this kit"), shadcn keeps `NotificationOverlay.svelte` and `types.ts` as its own files. Rejected: a class map (shadcn paints with `Card`, `Alert` and `Button`, not classes); the paint passed as a snippet from `+layout.svelte` (a shared file too, and tailwind does not modify it today); one card for each component (two modified files per level where one suffices: the version notice is a notification with actions).
- **K3-3, the root's push items**: the `event.data.notification` fix at the root's `core/service-worker/index.ts`, test-first (a node case of the message handler, red on `origin/main`); the worker's push parsing and click target as `web/src/service-worker/push.ts` (`notificationFromPush`, `notificationTarget`, from `with/push-notifications`' `$lib/push/payload.ts`) typed by the Declarative Web Push payload declared ONCE at the root, the standard's shape, with that file's tests; `with/push-notifications` then reads the root's functions, and keeps only a type test that `@push-notification/server`'s `DeclarativePushNotification` is assignable to the root's (its two `service-worker` entries go). And the root's unwired `core/service-worker/push-notifications/` dropped (and `with/push-notifications`' omission of it, and its README's pointer). Rejected: the root depending on `@push-notification/server` (a service's package at the root of a tree most of whose nodes have no push); replacing the module by `@push-notification/client` at the root (a dependency for a feature nothing there wires).
- **K3-4, the three format helpers to the root**: `core/utils/format/hex.ts`, `json.ts`, `error.ts` and their tests at template-svelte, with a root `index.ts` of the three; jolly-roger's `index.ts` adds `balance` and `value` (one entry: "the web3 formatters beside the root's"), so no reader is edited. Rejected: jolly-roger's barrel renamed (five readers edited in every descendant); left at jolly-roger (P1-4: a playground copying three plain helpers from a web3 template).
- **K3-5, the overlay layers at template-svelte-shadcn**: `core/ui/layers.ts` and `layers.test.ts`, the `--z-layer-*` scale in `app.css`, the containers in `+layout.svelte`, the four `*-content.svelte` portals and `NotificationOverlay.svelte`'s z-index move to shadcn, and `NavigationProgress.svelte`'s (a root file) becomes a shadcn entry. The seven layers keep their order; `system`'s reason says "modals whose visibility is derived from state the app holds" without jolly-roger's ADR. jolly-roger `main` loses six entries and its `app.css` and `+layout.svelte` entries lose the layers from their reasons. Rejected: the layers at the root (it has no portalled overlay: no bits-ui); the four patches moved without `layers.ts` (they import it); leaving them (every regenerated shadcn component below shadcn re-patched by hand, as their comments ask).
- **K3-6, the blog's posts source as one small file a site modifies**: template-svelte-tailwind-blog's `lib/blog/source.ts` holds the five things a site varies (the eager globs, `slugOf(path)`, `isPublished(post)`, `postURL(slug)`, and `Post` extending a base), and `lib/blog/posts.ts` and `lib/blog/feed.ts` (shared, tested in node) do the listing, sorting, excerpt, enclosure and RSS that `api/posts` and `feed.xml` now inline; the two routes become a few lines over them, the same at every site. The publish rule made one (`isPublished`, the blog's default `published !== false`). Each site then modifies `source.ts` (and `types.ts` if kept) instead of three files including the 194-line feed. Rejected: an alias like `$app-specific` at the blog (a mechanism for one file); the feed shared with template-svelte-docs at template-svelte-tailwind (dead code in tailwind and its other leaves, for about 40 lines).
- **K3-7, wabisabi-website's build leaves its tree clean**: `build` is `vite build`; the refresh is its own script (`release:refresh`), run by `pages.yml` before the build, so the deployed site still carries the latest release and a developer commits a refresh when they mean to. Rejected: a generated, gitignored file (the committed fallback is the seed an offline build and its test read); leaving it (offshoot-fanout skips a dirty node, so every cascade stops there).
- **K3-8, the contracts-page fixture at jolly-roger `main`**: `web/e2e/fixtures/contracts-page.ts` from template-zk-game's shape (`openContract(page, name)` by client-side navigation, `writeCard(page, fn)`, `sendWrite(page)`) with template-commit-reveal's `selectContract` reasoning and `writeForm`/`executeButton`, the contract from `MAIN_PAGE.contract` and the write from a new `MAIN_PAGE.write` (`{function, args}`) in `web/e2e/routes.ts`, the seam a node sets; `contracts.e2e.ts`, `escape-hatch.e2e.ts`, `pending-operation.e2e.ts` and `stalling-wallet.ts` read it. In K3d and K3e each descendant takes the stem's four files and fixture and sets `MAIN_PAGE.write` (template-commit-reveal `addToReserve`, template-zk-game `revokeDelegate` with its argument), dropping four entries each where nothing else differs. Rejected: the constants in the fixture (a descendant edits the fixture, a modified file again); two fixtures kept (K1-9's reason for the item).
- **K3-9, jolly-roger's smaller items, in K3c**: the faucet as a capability provided by `$lib/index.ts`, as P1-3 made the block explorer (`core/capabilities/`), so `core/ui/faucet` imports no `$env` and no deployments store at module level; the explorer's two views print `formatDecodedTransaction`'s label (and selector) instead of their own "Contract Call", test-first where a view's logic moves to `.ts`; "the game" made "the app" in `lib/embedded`, `lib/indexer`, their tests (`const GAME` there becomes `const CONTRACT`) and `run-e2e-tests.sh`; `routes.ts`' comment says what a node sets; `indexed.ts` says template-commit-reveal's store IS built on it. Rejected: the prose left to the next touch (every cascade re-carries it into the games).
- **K3-10, template-commit-reveal's items, in K3d**: its `+page.svelte` and `home.e2e.ts` read `MAIN_PAGE` (`route`, `homeLink`), so infinite-gambit drops its two entries; the hover seam moved from infinite-gambit to template-commit-reveal `main`, its two tests with it, test-first (red at `main` before), infinite-gambit's five entries gone; both boards pass `gate: core.chainFetchGate` (a behaviour change: the board waits for the chain as the skeleton's poll does), test-first in `placement/indexer.test.ts` and stratagems' board tests, full e2e of `with/indexer`, `with/fuzd-pixi-indexer` and stratagems; `offline.ts`' reason rewritten (the reference game's composition on `$lib/game/offline`). Rejected: the gate in its own later step (one more merge per indexed node for one line each).
- **K3-11, every remaining node writes its own list, in V1's format, in the step that crosses it**: K3a template-svelte-tailwind and template-svelte-shadcn (the root keeps none, as the check says); K3b the nine leaves; K3e template-zk-game, duel-in-the-dark, bleeps and mandalas (their merges first, so the list is written against the stems they then have). A child repo's list starts with no stem entries, as `check-divergences.mjs` says. Rejected: the lists in a step of their own after the seams (written against files the seams are about to change).
- **K3-12, voxlings stays out** (156 behind, the user's live work, untracked notes in its checkout): it gets K2 and K3, its list included, with its own next cascade, as V1, K1 and K2 left it. Rejected: cascading it here (156 commits of K-steps, against P-2).
- **K3-13, three items leave K3**: the splash stays reveal-or-die's (`with/pixi-js`' Decision 2: brand stays with the game; its quality fixes are the game's); the Router fix goes to `solidity-proxy` (a fixed release, after which infinite-gambit drops its vendored copy and its entries: a K4 item, since it is a dependency bump); F1-12's middleware stays stratagems' (no second publisher), its note carried. Rejected: moving the splash anyway (it would edit `+layout.svelte` at `with/pixi-js`, what its gate was designed to avoid).
- **K3-14, the measure**: every node's verify against its baseline (with `OFFSHOOT_STEM_REFS`), every node's `check-divergences` exit 0 (no node left at "no list of its own" but the root and voxlings), `check-omissions`, `check-stem-drift`. The svelte line has no e2e: its verify builds (`pnpm --filter ./web build`), and K3c's e2e is where the card and the layers are first seen in a browser. Full e2e: jolly-roger `main`, `with/embedded-chain`, `integration`, `with/push-notifications` (K3c); template-commit-reveal `main`, `with/indexer`, `with/fuzd-pixi-indexer`, stratagems and infinite-gambit (K3d); template-zk-game and bleeps (K3e); on every other node `layout-shell`, `overlays`, `home` and the four contracts-page suites, three at a time. No lockfile change is expected but K3-3's (none adds a dependency; dropping the root module adds none), so a `pnpmDepsHash` moves only if one does, recomputed as the prompt says. Rejected: a full e2e everywhere (about five hours for paint and fixtures).

**Start here** (K3a; the later steps the same, with their own repos and heads).

```sh
for r in template-svelte template-svelte-tailwind template-svelte-shadcn template-svelte-tailwind-blog conquest-website-2 ronan-eth wabisabi-website arts palestine conquest-demo template-svelte-docs rocketh-website jolly-roger template-commit-reveal reveal-or-die bomber-world stratagems infinite-gambit template-zk-game duel-in-the-dark bleeps mandalas voxlings; do git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/$r fetch --all --prune; echo "$r $(git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/$r rev-parse --short origin/main) dirty=$(git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/$r status --porcelain | wc -l)"; done
for r in template-svelte-tailwind template-svelte-shadcn; do git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/$r diff --stat stem/main origin/main -- web/src/lib/core/notifications web/src/lib/core/service-worker | cat; done   # tailwind +33 -164, +33 -198; shadcn the two plus NotificationCard, NotificationOverlay, types.ts, on 2026-11-22
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/template-svelte show origin/main:web/src/lib/core/service-worker/index.ts | grep -n 'fromPushNotification(event.data'   # :520 on 2026-11-22
git -C ~/dev/github/wighawag/jolly-roger show origin/main:.offshoot-divergences | grep -E 'shadcn/ui|NotificationOverlay|NavigationProgress'   # six on 2026-11-22
```

**Do, K3a** (preview branches `tf-K3a/main` of the three repos, the plan on `tf-K3a/work`; one commit per item, a merge counting as its own): 1. re-derive; each node's verify as the baseline. 2. At the root: K3-3's fix test-first, then `push.ts` and its tests, then the push module dropped; K3-4's helpers; K3-2's split (`view.ts` test-first, the card, the two components on it). 3. tailwind: the merge, its card in Tailwind UI classes (its two rewrites gone), its list. 4. shadcn: the merge, its card on `$ui/card` and `core/ui/button` (its two rewrites gone), K3-5's layers from jolly-roger `main` byte for byte where they are generic, its list. Every verify (with `OFFSHOOT_STEM_REFS`), `check-divergences` (0 at tailwind and shadcn), `check-omissions`, `check-stem-drift`.

**Do, K3b** (preview branches `tf-K3b/main` of the nine leaves): one merge per leaf of its stem's K3a (or K3b) head, top down (the blog before its sites, arts before palestine, template-svelte-docs before rocketh-website); at the blog K3-6 test-first (`posts.ts` and `feed.ts` in node), each site's `source.ts`; at wabisabi-website K3-7; each leaf's list; at each shadcn leaf (arts, palestine, conquest-demo, template-svelte-docs, rocketh-website) the layout takes shadcn's layers and drops `NotificationOverlay` (gone at shadcn, see "As built (K3a)"), and a leaf that styled the update notice or the notifications does it through the card's `class`/`classes`; every verify and check.

**Do, K3c** (preview branches `tf-K3c/<branch>` of jolly-roger's nine nodes): 1. the merge of shadcn's K3a head into `main` (the six entries gone, the layers the stem's; the layout's toast layer renders `<Notifications>` without `NotificationOverlay`, which K3a removed; the notice layer's striped `classes.root` becomes `classes.region` (the banner's strip; on the card it would be hidden); `json.test.ts` takes the root's and its "every call site uses it" guard moves to a file of jolly-roger's own, e.g. `json-call-sites.test.ts`, so no entry is needed; `core/utils/format/index.ts` adds `balance` and `value`, its one entry); 2. K3-8's fixture and `MAIN_PAGE.write`, the four suites on it; 3. K3-9's items, test-first where logic moves; 4. the cascade (`with/embedded-chain` and `with/indexer` take the prose, `with/push-notifications` the root's push functions and its type test, its two entries and its omission gone); every verify and check, the e2e of K3-14.

**Do, K3d** (preview branches `tf-K3d/<branch>`; the stem's names are K3c's "As built"): one merge per template-commit-reveal node of its stem's K3c head, the fixture and the four suites taken from the stem with `MAIN_PAGE.read` and `MAIN_PAGE.write` (`addToReserve`, its `args` turning the label into its two `uint256`s, `shownOnMainPage: false`) set in `web/e2e/routes.ts` and template-commit-reveal's own `fixtures/contracts-page.ts` deleted (its readers, the game suites, move to the stem's names); the layout's `NotificationOverlay` import and wrapper dropped and `provideFaucet(faucet)` kept beside `provideBlockExplorer`, and every `hasFaucet` reader (its `core.ts`, `TopUpModal.svelte`, `InsufficientFundsModal.svelte`, `game/core/commit-reveal-game.ts`) on `faucetConfigured`, as jolly-roger's; at `main` K3-10's menu, hover seam (test-first) and reason; at `with/indexer` the gate (test-first); the games' merges (infinite-gambit's hover and menu entries gone, stratagems' board gate); every verify and check, the e2e of K3-14.

**Do, K3e** (preview branches `tf-K3e/main`): template-zk-game's merge of `integration`'s head (K3c's, and K3d's two e2e hardenings from jolly-roger `main`, see "As built (K3d)"; also K2a's 14 commits), its fixture replaced by the stem's with its `MAIN_PAGE.read` (`lastMatchId`) and `MAIN_PAGE.write` (`revokeDelegate`, `args` its `inputFor` address from the label, `shownOnMainPage: false`), its layout's `NotificationOverlay` dropped and its faucet readers on `faucetConfigured` (as K3d's), its list; duel-in-the-dark's merge and list; bleeps' and mandalas' merges of `main`'s head (K3c's and K3d's) and their lists; every verify and check, the e2e of K3-14.

**Done when** (K3a): the root's two notification components are its behaviour over one `NotificationCard.svelte`, which tailwind and shadcn each modify alone; K3-3's case was red before; the root has no `push-notifications` module and has `push.ts` tested; the three helpers at the root; shadcn owns `layers.ts` and the four portals; tailwind and shadcn verify with `check-divergences` exit 0. (K3b): the nine leaves level with their stems, each list its own and the check 0; the blog's sites modify `source.ts` and no posts route or feed; wabisabi-website's verify leaves its tree clean. (K3c): jolly-roger `main` lists no `shadcn/ui` file, no `NotificationOverlay` or `NavigationProgress`; the four suites read the fixture; `core/ui/faucet` imports no `$env`; no "the game" in `lib/embedded` or `lib/indexer`; `with/push-notifications` lists neither service-worker file; e2e of K3-14 at its baseline. (K3d): no game modifies `+page.svelte`, `home.e2e.ts`, the hover files or the four suites for constants; the gate's cases were red before; e2e at baseline. (K3e): template-zk-game level with `integration`; the four lists their own, the check 0. Every step: every push approved; slot 0's ports free before and after; any lockfile change with its `pnpmDepsHash`.

**As built (K3a, 2026-11-22)**, on preview branches `tf-K3a/main` of template-svelte, template-svelte-tailwind and template-svelte-shadcn; where it differs from the decisions above, this is what was built (K3b and K3c build on these names):

- **K3-2, the seam**: the root's `core/notifications/view.ts` holds `notificationActions(notification, service)` (the notification's own action first and primary, then `dismiss`; `dismiss` alone and primary without one), `isUpdateAvailable(state)`, `UPDATE_NOTICE` (the root's wording: "A new version is available." / "Reload to get the update.") and `updateActions(serviceWorker)` (`Reload` primary, `Dismiss`), and the types `NotificationData`, `NotificationAction` and `NotificationClasses` (shadcn's members). `NotificationCard.svelte` takes `{notification, actions, class?, classes?}` and carries the fly transition, as shadcn's did. `Notifications.svelte` and `VersionAndInstallNotfications.svelte` each render the live region (root CSS, the stack top right from 640px) and cards; both take `class` and `classes` for the card, the version notice `src` as its icon (its `alt` prop is gone: the card's icon is `alt="icon"`, as shadcn's). The root card has no close X (dismiss is an action, as in shadcn's). The update notice takes `placement` (`NoticePlacement`, decided by the user 2026-11-22): `'banner'`, the DEFAULT, a strip across the top with the card laid out as one row (the card's `layout: 'card' | 'banner'`, `NotificationLayout`, which every kit's card honours), so it does not share the corner the notifications stack in; or `'corner'`, a card stacked like them. `classes.region` styles the banner's strip (only while the notice shows), which is where jolly-roger's stripes go. Where the banner and the notifications still meet (top right on desktop), the notification paints over the banner, the user's rule ("notifications are more important" than a reload): shadcn's toast layer is above its notice layer, and the root's layout renders the notice BEFORE `<Notifications>` for the same reason. Rejected: notifications at the bottom right (the toasts are there), the banner shortened to clear the corner.
- **K3-2 at shadcn, where it differs**: shadcn's `NotificationOverlay.svelte` and `types.ts` are GONE rather than kept. The root's components now hold the live region (K3-2), so wrapping `<Notifications>` in the overlay nested a second `aria-live` region and a second fixed box around the first, and the overlay's one other job, its `z-999`, is the toast layer's now (K3-5); `NotificationClasses` is the root's (`view.ts`), which a component prop at the root needs. shadcn's version notice stays a top banner by default (the card's banner layout, a row as its `Alert` was), with the root's wording ("Reload"/"Dismiss" for "Update Now"/"Later"). Every layout below shadcn imports `NotificationOverlay`, so each drops the import and the wrapper in its K3 merge.
- **K3-3**: `web/src/service-worker/push.ts` (the root's worker folder, beside `fetch-and-update-cache.ts`; its test `test/service-worker/push.test.ts`, `with/push-notifications`' `payload.test.ts` with its import changed) exports `DeclarativePushNotification`, `NotificationAction`, `PushedNotification`, `notificationFromPush` and `notificationTarget`, byte for byte `with/push-notifications`' `$lib/push/payload.ts` but the type declared instead of imported. The fix is byte for byte `with/push-notifications`' `core/service-worker/index.ts`, so that file merges clean there; its case `test/lib/core/service-worker/messages.test.ts` drives `createServiceWorker` with a fake `navigator`.
- **K3-4**: `hex.ts`, `json.ts`, `error.ts` and `hex.test.ts`, `error.test.ts` byte for byte jolly-roger `main`'s; `json.test.ts` keeps only the helpers' cases (jolly-roger's second `describe`, that every call site stringifying `args` or `metadata` uses the replacer, finds none at the root and fails its own "finds the call sites" guard), so K3c moves that guard to a file of jolly-roger's own.
- **K3-5**: `layers.ts` keeps the seven layers, ids and exports; its header's bug story and three `holds` lose jolly-roger's specifics (the top-up modal, ADR-0004, AcrossPages, the sending notice); `layers.test.ts` and the four portals are byte for byte jolly-roger's, as is `NavigationProgress.svelte`. `app.css`' block is jolly-roger's with its debug surfaces, ADR and test references taken out. shadcn's layout renders the containers from `LAYERS` with the update notice in `notice`, `<Notifications>` in `toast` and the progress bar in `progress`, which moved it after the page in document order.
- **Seen in a browser** (a dev server on 4183 and a headless Chromium, both stopped): two notifications at the root and at shadcn, cards top right, shadcn's inside `[data-layer="toast"]`, one live region each, no page error. The root's card inherits the browser's `<p>` margins (as the old panel did), left for a later paint pass.

**As built (K3b, 2026-11-22)**, on preview branches `tf-K3b/main` of the nine leaves; where it differs from the decisions above:

- **K3-6, the seam**: `lib/blog/posts.ts` exports `BasePost` (`title`, `slug`, `date`, `description?`, `image?`), `PostsSource<P>` (`modules`, `sources`, `slugOf`, `isPublished`, `postURL`), `listPosts` (published, newest first, as written: the api) and `feedPosts` (the same with the description falling back to an excerpt, then the title: the feed), and `excerptFrom`; `lib/blog/feed.ts` exports `hostOf`, `escapeXML`, `enclosureFor(image, {host, sizeOf})` and `renderFeed(channel, posts, {postURL, sizeOf})`, with the disk read (`statSync` under `static/`) left in the route as `sizeOf`, so both files are plain values in and out. `lib/blog/source.ts` exports `postsSource` and the site's `Post` (`BasePost` and its own fields); `lib/types.ts` is GONE at the blog (its one type moved to `source.ts`), so ronan-eth deletes it too and conquest-website-2 keeps it as its own file with its search and heading types only. The post page's import (`routes/blog/[slug]/+page.ts`) is not part of the seam: a site that changes its glob changes that import too, which `source.ts`' comment says (conquest-website-2's is in its own `routes/devlog/`). The built `feed.xml` and `api/posts` are byte for byte what they were at the blog and at both sites.
- **K3-7**: `release:refresh` at `web/package.json` (`node scripts/fallback-release.ts`) and at the root (`pnpm --filter ./web release:refresh`); `pages.yml` runs it, with the token, before `pnpm verify`; the README, the script's header and two comments say a build never runs it.
- **The update notices**: no shadcn leaf styled the notice or the notifications (arts' and conquest-demo's layouts are the stem's byte for byte; template-svelte-docs' is the stem's layers around its header, `<main>` and search dialog). wabisabi-website (a tailwind leaf) keeps its own `UpdateNotice.svelte`, dark on its paper and under its nav, rather than the root's notice on the kit's card: the kit's white card and its scoped region cannot be restyled into it through `classes`.
- **Found by the merge**: template-svelte-docs carries a shadcn `sheet` (its header's mobile navigation) that portalled to `body`, which shadcn's `layers.test.ts` caught; it now portals to `DRAWER_LAYER` (its own file, so no entry). Its home is template-svelte-shadcn, with the other generic components docs holds (the analysis' item); and template-svelte-shadcn's README still says the notifications render "via `NotificationCard` / `NotificationOverlay`", which K3a removed: both for the next change at shadcn.

**As built (K3c, 2026-11-22)**, on preview branches `tf-K3c/<branch>` of jolly-roger's nine nodes; where it differs from the decisions above (K3d and K3e build on these names):

- **The merge at `main`**: `layers.ts` and `json.test.ts` the stem's; `app.css` keeps one layer block, the stem's, with jolly-roger's notes beside it (its two debug surfaces, ADR-0004, the modal tests and the overlays e2e) and its `--navbar-height` in a `:root` of its own, so the entry's reason is jolly-roger's additions, not the layers; the layout keeps its own overlay comment and snippets (sonner, the striped notice through `classes.region`, the sending notice) on the stem's containers. `json.test.ts`' call-site guard is `test/lib/core/utils/format/json-call-sites.test.ts`, jolly-roger's own file. `web/README.md` drops its link to the root's removed push module.
- **K3-8, the seam**: `web/e2e/routes.ts`' `MAIN_PAGE` gains `read` (a view function, for the Read tab's suites: `getLastMessages`) and `write: MainPageWrite` (`function`, `args(label)` turning a suite's label into the form's values, one per input, and `shownOnMainPage`, whether the main page shows the label once mined, which the write suite then reads back). `web/e2e/fixtures/contracts-page.ts` exports `WRITE_CONTRACT`, `WRITE_FUNCTION`, `READ_FUNCTION`, `DEFAULT_LABEL`, `writeArgs`, `goToContractsPage` (a client-side navigation), `selectContract` (template-commit-reveal's settle-then-click, with a retried open), `openContract`, `functionCard`, `writeForm`, `executeButton`, `writeInput(page, i)` (by the inputs' `input-<i>` ids), `openWrite`, `fillWrite` and `sendWrite`. A DECISION the step had to take: the stalled send (`stalling-wallet.ts`' `sendAndStall`) and the inspector's send (`pending-operation.e2e.ts`) now go through `/contracts` on EVERY node, `main` included, which is what `with/local-signer` already did for the first, since the account executor is the one route that reaches the wallet whether or not a local signer posts; so `with/local-signer` loses its three entries (`stalling-wallet.ts`, `escape-hatch.e2e.ts`, `pending-operation.e2e.ts`: its inspector's send needs no browser authorisation any more, as template-zk-game's already said), the escape hatch's "releases the submit button" case is the Execute button's on every node, and its last case no longer looks for the greeting on the page (a dialog check, `with/local-signer`'s). `stalling-wallet.ts` no longer exports `writeForm` or `executeButton`: they are the fixture's.
- **K3-9, the faucet**: `core/ui/faucet/faucet.ts` (`Faucet = {api(), link()}`, read at each call, a blank value none; `createFaucet`, `faucetConfigured`), `core/capabilities/faucet.ts` (`provideFaucet`, `useFaucet`, optional), `$lib/index.ts`' `faucet` from `PUBLIC_FAUCET_API` and `PUBLIC_FAUCET_LINK`, provided by the layout; `hasFaucet`, `hasFaucetApi`, `hasFaucetLink` and the unread `getFaucetLink` gone. The faucet button, the navbar and the funds remedy read `faucetConfigured(useFaucet())`; at `with/local-signer` (and below) `TopUpModal.svelte` too, and `context/core.ts` gives the top-up flow `faucet.api()`, `faucet.link()` and `faucetConfigured(faucet)` from `$lib` (no `PUBLIC_FAUCET_*` import left there). Pinned by `test/lib/core/ui/faucet/faucet.test.ts` (6 cases, red first), which also asserts the folder imports neither `$env` nor the deployments store.
- **K3-9, the explorer**: `transactionHeadline(tx, decoded?)` in `transactionDecoder.ts` (`{kind: 'function' | 'creation' | 'other', label, details?}`: the decoder's label and selector once decoded, a transfer a Transfer; before, what the transaction says of itself), read by both views; `test/routes/explorer/transaction-headline.test.ts`, 8 cases, red first.
- **K3-9, the prose**: "the game" made the app's in `lib/embedded` (9 files and 2 tests, `offline.ts` one line) and `lib/indexer` (4 files; `follower.test.ts`' `toyGame` is `toyApp`, `worker-choice.test.ts`' `GAME` is `CONTRACT` and its prefix `myapp`, `host.test.ts`' path), `indexed.ts` saying template-commit-reveal's store is built on it, and the e2e scripts (`run-e2e-tests.sh`, `e2e-link-built.mjs`). Left as they are: the examples that name a descendant's game as one (a commit-reveal game's stake, a timed game's clock, template-commit-reveal's submission storage key).
- **K3-3 at `with/push-notifications`**: its `$lib/push/payload.ts` and `payload.test.ts` gone (the worker imports the root's `./push`), `onchain/notifier/greetings.ts` types its push by `@push-notification/server`'s `DeclarativePushNotification` directly, and `test/lib/push/payload-type.test.ts` (1 case) asserts the server's type is the root's (`expectTypeOf`, and an assignment `pnpm check` type-checks); its two service-worker entries, its `.offshoot-omissions` block and that file's entry gone; its README says the worker is the root's. Done in its merge of `with/indexer` (the worker conflicted there), the README in a commit of its own.
- **Two merges at `with/local-signer`**: `main` gained a comment rewrap after the first (5da86c1c), merged again rather than amended.

**As built (K3d, 2026-11-22)**, on preview branches `tf-K3d/<branch>` of template-commit-reveal's nine nodes and of reveal-or-die, bomber-world, stratagems and infinite-gambit; where it differs from the decisions above (K3e builds on these names):

- **The four suites and the fixture are the stem's byte for byte, at every node, and two hardenings went home.** template-commit-reveal's own versions carried hardening the stem's did not (in `contracts.e2e.ts` a poll that the write recorded an operation, in `escape-hatch.e2e.ts` a `test.slow()`, in `pending-operation.e2e.ts` the operation instead of the navbar badge, the drawer scoped to its layer, a wait on `wallet-status` and a 240 s budget). The stem's passed at template-commit-reveal `main` as they were (25 in the five suites, then the full 63), so they were taken; then bomber-world's full run failed the stem's inspector suite exactly as the template's comment said it would (`getByRole('dialog')` meeting a modal still closing: a strict-mode violation, and the retry toggling the drawer shut). So two of the hardenings were made at their home, jolly-roger `main` (91259bac): `pending-operation.e2e.ts` takes the drawer in `#--layer-drawer` and opens it only while it is shut, and `contracts.e2e.ts` polls that the write recorded an operation before `waitForTransaction` (which is also true of a page that sent nothing, and was all a node with `shownOnMainPage: false` asserted); cascaded clean to jolly-roger's eight other nodes and merged again into every K3d node. The rest (`test.slow()`, the badge, the 240 s) were not needed and are gone.
- **`MAIN_PAGE` below jolly-roger**: template-commit-reveal `main` sets `read: 'getCycleNumber'` and `write: {function: 'addToReserve', args: (label) => [labelAsPlayer(label), '0'], shownOnMainPage: false}` in `web/e2e/routes.ts`, the player being the label's 32-bit FNV-1a hash in decimal (`labelAsPlayer`, beside it: the identity is a `uint256`, a zero amount moves nothing). reveal-or-die (and bomber-world by cascade) sets `write` to `revokeDelegate` with `labelAsAddress(label)` (the hash in an address's low bytes, one for a hash of zero, the value the call rejects); stratagems sets `contract: 'Stratagems'` and keeps the template's read and write; infinite-gambit sets nothing (its contract is `Game`, with `addToReserve` and `getCycleNumber`). Entries: template-commit-reveal `main` loses four (`stalling-wallet.ts`, `contracts.e2e.ts`, `escape-hatch.e2e.ts`, `pending-operation.e2e.ts`; `routes.ts` keeps its one, reworded), reveal-or-die five for one (`routes.ts`), stratagems one for one (`contracts-page.ts` for `routes.ts`).
- **Burner accounts**: the stem's inspector suite takes burner 2, which template-commit-reveal's `out-of-gas.e2e.ts` held, so that suite takes 3 (the one the inspector vacated); in reveal-or-die and bomber-world it arrives so in their renamed `signer-out-of-gas.e2e.ts`, and stratagems' own `faucet.e2e.ts` moves from 2 to 3 for the same reason.
- **K3-10, the menu**: `routes/+page.svelte`'s first entry links `MAIN_PAGE.route` and says `MAIN_PAGE.homeLink` (from `$app-specific`), and `home.e2e.ts` reads `MAIN_PAGE` of `e2e/routes.ts`; `home.e2e.ts` keeps its entry (the canvas, the Offline entry). infinite-gambit NEVER modified `home.e2e.ts` (the measure was wrong there), and its `+page.svelte` is its own landing, so that entry stays.
- **K3-10, the hover seam**: the three framework hunks byte for byte infinite-gambit's, with `input.svelte.test.ts`' three cases (2 red before) and a new case in `commit-reveal-game.test.ts` (red before: the skeleton hands a hover only while ready, puts it out when readiness drops, and never after stop). infinite-gambit loses four entries, not five: `canvas2d.svelte.test.ts` is chess's renderer's test and stays.
- **K3-10, the gate**: `createIndexedBoard` (the reference game's and stratagems') takes `gate` and the compositions pass `core.chainFetchGate`; pinned at template-commit-reveal by a case of `placement/indexer.test.ts` on a real fold (no host while closed), at stratagems by `test/lib/stratagems/indexed-board-gate.test.ts` (the board hands the indexed state its gate; the indexed state's half is `indexed-state.test.ts`'), both red before. The e2e sets `PUBLIC_NODE_URL`, so `chainFetchGate` is `undefined` there and the gate is not exercised by any browser suite.
- **The faucet's readers**: `context/core.ts` resolved to the stem's (`faucet` from `$lib`), and `game/core/commit-reveal-game.ts` reads `faucetConfigured(faucet)` from `$lib` too; the layout's `NotificationOverlay` went in a clean merge, and no game's layout imported it.
- **`upstream/with/indexer`** (not named by the Do) took jolly-roger `with/indexer`'s K3c head, its list unchanged, so `with/indexer` merged both stems.

**As built (K3e, 2026-11-22)**, on preview branches `tf-K3e/main` of template-zk-game, duel-in-the-dark, bleeps and mandalas; where it differs from the decisions above:

- **template-zk-game**: the fixture, `stalling-wallet.ts` and the three suites are the stem's byte for byte; `web/e2e/routes.ts` sets `read: 'lastMatchId'` and `write: {function: 'revokeDelegate', args: (label) => [labelAsAddress(label)], shownOnMainPage: false}`, `labelAsAddress` being the old `inputFor` (the first 20 bytes of the label's SHA-256, `node:crypto`). Its layout and faucet readers needed nothing: the layout never imported `NotificationOverlay` and no reader of `hasFaucet` is its own, so the merge took both from the stem. The merge also brought K2a's opener, `$lib/offline-world.ts` and its test, and `offline-demo.e2e.ts`: the opener reads `$lib/offline-node`, which this template omits with `$lib/offline.ts` and `/offline-demo`, so all three are omitted with them (three new `.offshoot-omissions` entries); `ui/delegation/authorise-here.ts` (K2-4) is kept, read by nothing here but generic and tested.
- **duel-in-the-dark** inherits template-zk-game's `read` and `write` (Duel is a `ZKMatches`), so its `routes.ts` changes only the route, heading, link and contract; its `.offshoot-omissions` kept its own side (its stem's three new entries are absent from its stem, so they cannot be omissions here).
- **bleeps' write is `delegate(address delegatee)`, not the `setApprovalForAll` its suites sent before**, and its read `contractURI`. `setApprovalForAll` takes a `bool`, which `/contracts` renders as a `<select>`, and the stem's `fillWrite` fills every input with `.fill()`, which Playwright refuses on a `<select>`. `delegate` cannot revert and moves no votes for an account that holds no Bleep, so the stem's fixture, `stalling-wallet.ts` and `escape-hatch.e2e.ts` are taken byte for byte. bleeps KEEPS its own `contracts.e2e.ts` (an entry): the stem's write case needs the `waitForTransaction` fixture, which bleeps' `fixtures/test.ts` deliberately does not have (no honest pending flag to wait on yet). `pending-operation.e2e.ts` stays omitted, though its reason ("the e2e drives `/demo`") no longer holds: taking it back brings its burner claim and `e2e-account-claims.test.ts`, which bleeps also omits (a K4 item).
- **mandalas keeps its own `stalling-wallet.ts` and `escape-hatch.e2e.ts`** (entries): its only harmless write is `setApprovalForAll`, the same `bool` `fillWrite` cannot fill, and its e2e is its own generation (plain Playwright, no `fixtures/test`, no burner list). Its `routes.ts` still sets `read` and `write` (the stem's fixture, which it now carries unread, type-checks against them) and says why its own fixture sends it. Its `app.css` keeps its `--navbar-height: var(--header-height)` in the stem's new jolly-roger `:root`, and its layout keeps its comment beside `provideFaucet(faucet)`.
